function _G.SQR.safeCancel(p1)
  if not p1 then
    return
  end

  pcall(function()
    if type(coroutine.close) == "function" then
      coroutine.close(p1)
    end
  end)
end

local sqr4 = _G.SQR
sqr4._hdCommandRemote = nil
sqr4._hdCommandQueue = {}
sqr4._hdCommandWorkerAlive = false
sqr4._hdCommandQueueMax = 40

local function f1()
  local hdCommandRemote = sqr4._hdCommandRemote

  if hdCommandRemote and hdCommandRemote.Parent then
    return hdCommandRemote
  end

  local hdAdminHDClient = sqr4.rs:FindFirstChild("HDAdminHDClient")

  if hdAdminHDClient then
    local signals = hdAdminHDClient:FindFirstChild("Signals")
    local requestCommandSilent = signals and signals:FindFirstChild("RequestCommandSilent")

    if requestCommandSilent and requestCommandSilent:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
      sqr4._hdCommandRemote = requestCommandSilent
      return requestCommandSilent
    end
  end

  for _, value5 in ipairs(sqr4.rs:GetDescendants()) do
    if value5:IsA("RemoteEvent") or value5:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
      local lower = value5.Name:lower()

      if lower:find("command", 1, true) or lower:find("exec", 1, true)
        or lower:find("silent", 1, true) then
        sqr4._hdCommandRemote = value5
        return value5
      end
    end
  end

  return nil
end

function sqr4.sendCommand(p2)
  local v1 = p2
  v1 = tostring(v1 or "")

  if v1 == "" then
    return false
  end

  local ok, result = pcall(function()
    local instance = f1()

    if not instance or not instance.Parent then
      return false
    end

    if instance:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
      instance:InvokeServer(v1)
    else
      instance:FireServer(v1)
    end

    return true
  end)

  if not ok then
    sqr4._hdCommandRemote = nil
  end

  return ok and result == true
end

function sqr4.sendCommandAsync(p3)
  local v2 = tostring(p3 or "")

  if v2 == "" then
    return
  end

  local hdCommandQueue = sqr4._hdCommandQueue

  if #hdCommandQueue >= sqr4._hdCommandQueueMax then
    table.remove(hdCommandQueue, 1)
  end

  hdCommandQueue[#hdCommandQueue + 1] = v2

  if sqr4._hdCommandWorkerAlive then
    return
  end

  sqr4._hdCommandWorkerAlive = true

  task.spawn(function()
    while sqr4._hdCommandWorkerAlive do
      local removed = table.remove(hdCommandQueue, 1)

      if not removed then
        sqr4._hdCommandWorkerAlive = false
        break
      end

      pcall(function() sqr4.sendCommand(removed) end)
      task.wait(0.01)
    end
  end)
end

local function f2()
  if typeof(request) == "function" then
    return request
  end

  if typeof(http_request) == "function" then
    return http_request
  end

  if typeof(syn) == "table" and typeof(syn.request) == "function" then
    return syn.request
  end
end

local function f3()
  return {
    apikey = sqr5.SUPABASE_KEY,
    Authorization = "Bearer " .. sqr5.SUPABASE_KEY,
    ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
    Prefer = "return=representation",
  }
end

local function f4(method, url, jsonEncode)
  local v3 = f2()

  if not v3 then
    return false, nil, "request function not found"
  end

  local v4 = { Url = url, Method = method, Headers = f3() }

  if jsonEncode then
    v4.Body = jsonEncode
  end

  local ok2, result2 = pcall(function() return v3(v4) end)

  if not ok2 or not result2 then
    return false, nil, tostring(result2 or "request failed")
  end

  local v5 = tonumber(result2.StatusCode) or 0
  local v6 = tostring(result2.Body or "")

  if v5 < 200 or v5 >= 300 then
    return false, v6, "HTTP " .. tostring(v5) .. " " .. v6
  end

  return true, v6, nil
end

local function f5()
  local v7, v8 = f4("GET", sqr5.SUPABASE_STATE_URL .. "?id=eq.1&select=data")

  if not v7 then
    return nil
  end

  local ok3, result3 = pcall(function() return sqr5.HttpService:JSONDecode(v8 or "") end)

  if ok3 and type(result3) == "table" and result3[1] and type(result3[1].data) == "table" then
    return result3[1].data
  end
end

function sqr5.putCloudRecord(data)

  local jsonEncode2 = sqr5.HttpService:JSONEncode({
    data = data,
    updated_at = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
  })

  return (f4("PATCH", sqr5.SUPABASE_STATE_URL .. "?id=eq.1", jsonEncode2))
end

      sqr5.rankState.owners[value7:lower()] = true
      table.insert(sqr5.OWNER_LIST, value7)
    end
  end

  for key2, value8 in pairs(v10.vips) do
    if value8 and not sqr5.rankState.owners[tostring(key2):lower()] then
      local vips = sqr5.rankState.vips
      local text3 = tostring(key2)
      vips[text3:lower()] = true
      table.insert(sqr5.VIP_LIST, tostring(key2))
    end
  end

  for key3, value9 in pairs(v10.bannedUsers) do
    if value9 then
      local bans = sqr5.rankState.bans
      local text4 = tostring(key3)
      bans[text4:lower()] = true
      table.insert(sqr5.BAN_LIST, tostring(key3))
    end
  end

  local name2 = sqr5.player.Name
  sqr5.isOwner = sqr5.rankState.owners[name2:lower()] == true
  local v11 = not sqr5.isOwner
  local isVIP = v11

  if v11 then
    local name3 = sqr5.player.Name
    isVIP = sqr5.rankState.vips[name3:lower()] == true
  end

  sqr5.isVIP = isVIP
  local name4 = sqr5.player.Name
  sqr5.isBanned = sqr5.rankState.bans[name4:lower()] == true
  return v10
end

function sqr5.isPlayerOwner(p6)
  if not p6 then
    return false
  end

  local owners3 = sqr5.rankState.owners
  local v13 = owners3

  if owners3 then
    local name5 = p6.Name
    v13 = sqr5.rankState.owners[name5:lower()]
  end

  return v13 == true or p6.Name == "nanMeWellDoit" or p6.Name == "nanNeverLet"
end

function sqr5.isPlayerVIP(p7)
  if not p7 then
    return false
  end

  local v14 = sqr5.isPlayerOwner(p7)
  local v15 = v14

  if not v14 then
    local vips2 = sqr5.rankState.vips
    local v16 = vips2

    if vips2 then
      local name6 = p7.Name
      v16 = sqr5.rankState.vips[name6:lower()]
    end

    v15 = v16 == true
  end

  return v15
end

function sqr5.getPlayerRank(p8)
  if sqr5.isPlayerOwner(p8) then
    return "Owner"
  end

  if sqr5.isPlayerVIP(p8) then
    return "VIP"
  end

  return "Free"
end

function sqr5.canTargetPlayer(p9)
  if not p9 or p9 == sqr5.player then
    return false
  end

  if sqr5.isPlayerSafe and sqr5.isPlayerSafe(p9) then
    return false
  end

  if sqr5.isPlayerOwner(p9) then
    return false
  end

  if sqr5.isVIP and sqr5.isPlayerVIP(p9) then
    return false
  end

  return true
end

local sqr7 = _G.SQR

function sqr7.addButtonFeedback(instance2)
  if not instance2 or not instance2:IsA("GuiButton") then
    return
  end

  if instance2:GetAttribute("SQRFeedback") then
    return
  end

  instance2:SetAttribute("SQRFeedback", true)

  local sqrButtonClick = Instance.new("Sound")
  sqrButtonClick.Name = "SQRButtonClick"
  sqrButtonClick.SoundId = sqr7.SQR_BUTTON_SOUND_ID
  sqrButtonClick.Volume = 0.35
  sqrButtonClick.Parent = instance2

  local size = instance2.Size
  local create
  local create2

  instance2.Activated:Connect(function()
    if not instance2 or not instance2.Parent then
      return
    end

    pcall(function()
      sqrButtonClick.SoundId = sqr7.SQR_BUTTON_SOUND_ID
      sqrButtonClick:Play()
    end)

    pcall(function()
      if create then
        create:Cancel()
      end
    end)

    pcall(function()
      if create2 then
        create2:Cancel()
      end
    end)

    instance2.Size = size
    local new = UDim2.new
    local scale = size.X.Scale
    local rounded = math.floor(size.X.Offset * 0.97)
    local scale2 = size.Y.Scale
    local floor = math.floor
    local offset = size.Y.Offset
    local size2 = new(scale * 0.97, rounded, scale2 * 0.92, floor(offset * 0.92))

    create = sqr7.TweenService:Create(instance2, TweenInfo.new(
      0.055, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    ), { Size = size2 })

    create2 = sqr7.TweenService:Create(instance2, TweenInfo.new(
      0.11, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    ), { Size = size })

    create:Play()

    create.Completed:Connect(function()
      if instance2 and instance2.Parent and create2 then
        create2:Play()
      end
    end)
  end)
end

function sqr7.installButtonFeedback(instance3)
  if not instance3 then
    return
  end

  for _, value10 in ipairs(instance3:GetDescendants()) do
    if value10:IsA("GuiButton") then
      sqr7.addButtonFeedback(value10)
    end
  end

  instance3.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
    if descendant:IsA("GuiButton") then
      pcall(function() sqr7.addButtonFeedback(descendant) end)
    end
  end)
end

local sqr9 = _G.SQR
sqr9.BangActive = false
sqr9.HeadBangActive = false
sqr9.bangTask = nil
sqr9.headbangTask = nil
sqr9.danceAnimationTrack = nil
sqr9.BangSpeed = 1
sqr9.BangDistance = 200
sqr9.BangHeight = 0
sqr9.BangOscillation = 1.5
sqr9.HeadBangSpeed = 1
sqr9.HeadBangDistance = 200
sqr9.HeadBangHeight = 0
sqr9.HeadBangOscillation = 1.5
sqr9.OriginalPositions = {}

function sqr9.SaveOriginalPosition(key4)
  local humanoidRootPart = sqr9.player.Character
    and sqr9.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

  if humanoidRootPart then
    sqr9.OriginalPositions[key4] = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
  end
end

function sqr9.RestoreOriginalPosition(key5)
  local cframe = sqr9.OriginalPositions[key5]

  if cframe then
    local humanoidRootPart2 = sqr9.player.Character
      and sqr9.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart2 then
      humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = cframe
      sqr9.OriginalPositions[key5] = nil
    end
  end
end

function sqr9.PlayDanceAnimation(p16)
  local humanoid = sqr9.player.Character
    and sqr9.player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

  if humanoid then
    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

    if animator then
      if sqr9.danceAnimationTrack then
        sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:Stop()
      end

      local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
      animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://5918726674"

      sqr9.danceAnimationTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
      sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:Play()
      sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(p16 or 1)
    end
  end
end

function sqr9.StopDanceAnimation()
  if sqr9.danceAnimationTrack then
    sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:Stop()
    sqr9.danceAnimationTrack = nil
  end
end

function sqr9.calculateBangMovement(p17, p18, p19)
  local v17 = math.clamp(sqr9.BangDistance, 1, 500) / 100
  local v18 = math.clamp(sqr9.BangHeight, 0, 1000) / 100
  local clamped = math.clamp(sqr9.BangOscillation, 1, 10)
  local v19 = (p19 or 0) + 0.1 * math.max(sqr9.BangSpeed, 0.1)
  local v20 = math.sin(v19)
  local lookVector = p17.CFrame.LookVector
  local v21 = p17.Position - lookVector * (v17 + v20 * v17 * clamped * 0.5)

  if (v21 - p17.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
    v21 = p17.Position - lookVector * 10
  end

  local vector = Vector3.new(0, v18, 0)
  p18.CFrame = CFrame.new(v21 + vector, p17.Position)
  return v19
end

function sqr9.StartBang(p20)
  if sqr9.BangActive or not p20 then
    return
  end

  sqr9.BangActive = true
  sqr9.SaveOriginalPosition("Bang")
  sqr9.PlayDanceAnimation(sqr9.BangSpeed)

  local v22 = 0

  sqr9.bangTask = task.spawn(function()
    while sqr9.BangActive and p20 and p20.Character do
      pcall(function()
        local humanoidRootPart3 = p20.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local humanoidRootPart4 = sqr9.player.Character
          and sqr9.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart3 and humanoidRootPart4 then
          v22 = sqr9.calculateBangMovement(humanoidRootPart3, humanoidRootPart4, v22)

          if sqr9.danceAnimationTrack then
            sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(math.clamp(sqr9.BangSpeed * (1 + math.clamp(
              sqr9.BangOscillation, 1, 10
            ) * 0.2), 1, 15))
          end
        end
      end)

      task.wait(0.03)
    end

    sqr9.StopDanceAnimation()
    sqr9.BangActive = false
    sqr9.RestoreOriginalPosition("Bang")
  end)
end

function sqr9.StartHeadBang(p21)
  if sqr9.HeadBangActive or not p21 then
    return
  end

  sqr9.HeadBangActive = true
  sqr9.SaveOriginalPosition("HeadBang")
  sqr9.PlayDanceAnimation(sqr9.HeadBangSpeed)

  local total = 0

  sqr9.headbangTask = task.spawn(function()
    while sqr9.HeadBangActive and p21 and p21.Character do
      pcall(function()
        local humanoidRootPart5 = p21.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local head = p21.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

        local humanoidRootPart6 = sqr9.player.Character
          and sqr9.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart5 and humanoidRootPart6 then
          local v23 = math.clamp(sqr9.HeadBangDistance, 1, 1000) / 100
          local v24 = math.clamp(sqr9.HeadBangHeight, 0, 1000) / 100
          local clamped2 = math.clamp(sqr9.HeadBangOscillation, 1, 10)
          total = total + 0.1 * sqr9.HeadBangSpeed
          local v25 = math.sin(total) * v23 * clamped2 * 0.5

          local position = head and head.Position
            or humanoidRootPart5.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)

          local lookVector2 = humanoidRootPart5.CFrame.LookVector
          local v26 = position + lookVector2 * (v23 + v25)

          if (v26 - position).Magnitude > 10 then
            v26 = position + lookVector2 * 10
          end

          local vector2 = Vector3.new(0, v24, 0)
          humanoidRootPart6.CFrame = CFrame.new(v26 + vector2, position)

          if sqr9.danceAnimationTrack then
            sqr9.danceAnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(math.clamp(sqr9.HeadBangSpeed
              * (1 + clamped2 * 0.2), 1, 15))
          end
        end
      end)

      task.wait(0.03)
    end

    sqr9.StopDanceAnimation()
    sqr9.HeadBangActive = false
    sqr9.RestoreOriginalPosition("HeadBang")
  end)
end

function sqr9.StopBang()
  sqr9.BangActive = false
  sqr9.HeadBangActive = false

  if sqr9.bangTask then
    pcall(function() task.cancel(sqr9.bangTask) end)
    sqr9.bangTask = nil
  end

  if sqr9.headbangTask then
    pcall(function() task.cancel(sqr9.headbangTask) end)
    sqr9.headbangTask = nil
  end

  sqr9.StopDanceAnimation()
  sqr9.RestoreOriginalPosition("Bang")
  sqr9.RestoreOriginalPosition("HeadBang")
end

sqr9.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
  if sqr9.BangActive or sqr9.HeadBangActive then
    wait(1)

    if sqr9.BangActive then
      sqr9.PlayDanceAnimation(sqr9.BangSpeed)
    elseif sqr9.HeadBangActive then
      sqr9.PlayDanceAnimation(sqr9.HeadBangSpeed)
    end
  end
end)

local sqr10 = _G.SQR
sqr10.AntiAFK = false
sqr10.afkConnection = nil

function sqr10.EnableAntiAFK()
  if sqr10.AntiAFK then
    return
  end

  sqr10.AntiAFK = true

  sqr10.afkConnection = sqr10.player.Idled:Connect(function()
    sqr10.VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.W, false, game)
    wait(0.1)
    sqr10.VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.W, false, game)
  end)

  spawn(function()
    while sqr10.AntiAFK do
      wait(10)

      pcall(function()
        local humanoid2 = sqr10.player.Character
          and sqr10.player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoid2 then
          humanoid2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        end
      end)
    end
  end)
end

function sqr10.DisableAntiAFK()
  sqr10.AntiAFK = false

  if sqr10.afkConnection then
    sqr10.afkConnection:Disconnect()
    sqr10.afkConnection = nil
  end
end

function sqr10.getNearPlayers()
  local list = {}
  local character = sqr10.player.Character

  if not character then
    return list
  end

  local humanoidRootPart7 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

  if not humanoidRootPart7 then
    return list
  end

  for _, value11 in ipairs(sqr10.Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if value11 ~= sqr10.player and value11.Character then
      local humanoidRootPart8 = value11.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if humanoidRootPart8 then
        if (humanoidRootPart7.Position - humanoidRootPart8.Position).Magnitude < 50 then
          table.insert(list, value11)
        end
      end
    end
  end

  return list
end

sqr15.spam1Boxes = { sqr15.msgBox }
sqr15.isRunning1 = false
sqr15.loop1 = nil

local function f8(p24)
  if not p24 or p24 == "" then
    return false
  end

  local v30 = false

  pcall(function()
    local textChannels = textChatService and textChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")

    local rbxGeneral = textChannels
      and (textChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
        or textChannels:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextChannel"))

    if rbxGeneral and typeof(rbxGeneral.SendAsync) == "function" then
      v30 = pcall(function() rbxGeneral:SendAsync(tostring(p24)) end)
    end
  end)

  if v30 then
    return true
  end

  pcall(function()
    if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
      setclipboard(tostring(p24))
    end

    task.wait(0.03)

    if sqr15.VIM then
      sqr15.VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Slash, false, game)
      task.wait(0.08)
      sqr15.VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Slash, false, game)
      task.wait(0.08)
      sqr15.VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Return, false, game)
      task.wait(0.08)
      sqr15.VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Return, false, game)
      v30 = true
    end
  end)

  return v30
end

local function f9()
  task.defer(function()
    sqr15.spam1List.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
      0, 0, 0, sqr15.spam1Layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10
    )
  end)
end

function sqr15.startSpam1()
  if sqr15.isRunning1 then
    return
  end

  local list2 = {}

  for _, value16 in ipairs(sqr15.spam1Boxes) do
    if tostring(value16.Text or "") ~= "" then
      table.insert(list2, value16.Text)
    end
  end

  if #list2 == 0 then
    return
  end

  local clamped4 = math.clamp(tonumber(sqr15.speedBox1.Text) or 0.5, 0.1, 3)

  sqr15.isRunning1 = true
  sqr15.sendButton1.Text = "Stop"
  sqr15.sendButton1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
  sqr15.spam1StartButton.Text = "Stop"
  sqr15.spam1StartButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 0)

  sqr15.loop1 = task.spawn(function()
    local v31 = 1

    while sqr15.isRunning1 do
      f8(list2[v31])
      task.wait(clamped4)
      v31 = v31 + 1

      if v31 > #list2 then
        list2 = {}
        v31 = 1

        for _, value17 in ipairs(sqr15.spam1Boxes) do
          if tostring(value17.Text or "") ~= "" then
            table.insert(list2, value17.Text)
          end
        end

        if #list2 == 0 then
          break
        end
      end
    end

    sqr15.isRunning1 = false
    sqr15.sendButton1.Text = "Send"
    sqr15.sendButton1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
    sqr15.spam1StartButton.Text = "Start"
    sqr15.spam1StartButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)
  end)
end

function sqr15.stopSpam1()
  sqr15.isRunning1 = false

  if sqr15.loop1 then
    pcall(function() sqr15.safeCancel(sqr15.loop1) end)
    sqr15.loop1 = nil
  end

  sqr15.sendButton1.Text = "Send"
  sqr15.sendButton1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  sqr15.spam1StartButton.Text = "Start"
  sqr15.spam1StartButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)
end

sqr15.sendButton1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  if sqr15.isRunning1 then
    sqr15.stopSpam1()
  else
    sqr15.startSpam1()
  end
end)

sqr15.spam1StartButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  if sqr15.isRunning1 then
    sqr15.stopSpam1()
  else
    sqr15.startSpam1()
  end
end)

sqr15.spam1PlusButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  local textBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
  textBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 35)
  textBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  textBox.BorderSizePixel = 0

local function makeTextButton2(text7, p27, p28, p29, p30, text8, p31, p32)
  local textButton4 = Instance.new("TextButton")
  textButton4.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 35)
  textButton4.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, p27)
  textButton4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  textButton4.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
  textButton4.BorderSizePixel = 0
  textButton4.Text = text7
  textButton4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
  textButton4.TextSize = 12
  textButton4.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
  textButton4.ZIndex = 5
  textButton4.Parent = parent2

  Instance.new("UICorner", textButton4).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)

  local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
  frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 10)
  frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(110, p27 + 13)
  frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 80)
  frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
  frame.Active = true
  frame.ZIndex = 5
  frame.Parent = parent2

  Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

  local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
  frame2.Size = UDim2.new(p30, 0, 1, 0)
  frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)
  frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
  frame2.ZIndex = 5
  frame2.Parent = frame

  Instance.new("UICorner", frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

  local frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")
  frame3.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
  frame3.Position = UDim2.new(p30, -9, 0.5, -9)
  frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)
  frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
  frame3.ZIndex = 6
  frame3.Parent = frame

  Instance.new("UICorner", frame3).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

  local function f10(clamped5)
    local clamped6 = math.clamp(clamped5, 0, 1)
    frame2.Size = UDim2.new(clamped6, 0, 1, 0)
    frame3.Position = UDim2.new(clamped6, -9, 0.5, -9)
    local rounded2 = math.floor(p28 + (p29 - p28) * clamped6 + 0.5)
    textButton4.Text = text8(rounded2)

    if p31 then
      p31(rounded2)
    end
  end

  local v32

  if not p32 then
    v32 = false

    frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input7)
      if input7.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input7.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        v32 = true

        f10(math.clamp((input7.Position.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X)
          / math.max(frame.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1))
      end
    end)

    sqr16.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input8)
      if v32
        and (input8.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
          or input8.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        f10(math.clamp((input8.Position.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X)
          / math.max(frame.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1))
      end
    end)

    sqr16.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input9)
      if input9.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input9.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        v32 = false
      end
    end)
  end

  return textButton4, frame, frame2, frame3
end

local function f11(x)
  local clamped7 = math.clamp((x - sqr16.speedSlider.AbsolutePosition.X)
    / math.max(sqr16.speedSlider.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)

  local v34 = 0.166
  local distance = math.abs(clamped7 - v34)

  for i = 2, #v33 do
    local distance2 = math.abs(clamped7 - v33[i])

    if distance2 < distance then
      distance = distance2
      v34 = v33[i]
    end
  end

  sqr16.speedFill.Size = UDim2.new(v34, 0, 1, 0)
  sqr16.speedKnob.Position = UDim2.new(v34, -9, 0.5, -9)

  local v35 = 1

  if v34 >= 0.75 then
    v35 = 3
  elseif v34 >= 0.35 then
    v35 = 2
  end

  sqr16.speedLabel.Text = "Speed: x" .. v35
  sqr16.BangSpeed = v35
  sqr16.HeadBangSpeed = v35

  if sqr16.BangActive and sqr16.danceAnimationTrack then
    sqr16.danceAnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(v35)
  end

  if sqr16.HeadBangActive and sqr16.danceAnimationTrack then
    sqr16.danceAnimationTrack:AdjustSpeed(v35)
  end
end

local list3 = {
  "كلبشه هيلوكيتي", "كلبشه ذهبيه", "ككورومي",
  "كلبشه بات مان",
}

local function getFindFirstChild()
  local character2 = sqr16.player.Character

  if not character2 then
    return nil
  end

  local backpack = sqr16.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")

  for _, value18 in ipairs(list3) do
    local findFirstChild3 = character2:FindFirstChild(value18)
      or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(value18)

    if findFirstChild3 then
      return findFirstChild3
    end
  end

  return nil
end

local function f12(selectedPlayer)
  if sqr16.cuffInProgress then
    return false
  end

  if not selectedPlayer or selectedPlayer == sqr16.player or not selectedPlayer.Parent then
    return false
  end

  if not selectedPlayer.Character then
    return false
  end

  sqr16.cuffInProgress = true
  local flag3 = false

  local ok4 = pcall(function()
    local character3 = sqr16.player.Character
    local humanoid3 = character3 and character3:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humanoidRootPart9 = character3 and character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not character3 or not humanoid3 or not humanoidRootPart9 then
      return
    end

    local findFirstChild4 = getFindFirstChild()

    if not findFirstChild4 then
      return
    end

    if findFirstChild4.Parent == sqr16.player.Backpack then
      humanoid3:EquipTool(findFirstChild4)
      task.wait(0.05)
    end

    local remoteEvent = findFirstChild4:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent")

    if not remoteEvent or not remoteEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
      return
    end

    local humanoidRootPart10 = selectedPlayer.Character
      and selectedPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not humanoidRootPart10 then
      return
    end

    if (humanoidRootPart9.Position - humanoidRootPart10.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
      local cframe2 = humanoidRootPart9.CFrame
      local v36 = tick()

      while tick() - v36 < 0.4 do
        if not selectedPlayer.Parent or not selectedPlayer.Character then
          break
        end

        local humanoidRootPart11 = selectedPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not humanoidRootPart11 or not humanoidRootPart9.Parent then
          break
        end

        humanoidRootPart9.CFrame = humanoidRootPart11.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0.5)
        remoteEvent:FireServer("Cuff", humanoidRootPart11)
        task.wait(0.05)
      end

      if humanoidRootPart9.Parent then
        humanoidRootPart9.CFrame = cframe2
      end
    else
      remoteEvent:FireServer("Cuff", humanoidRootPart10)
    end

    flag3 = true
  end)

  sqr16.cuffInProgress = false
  return ok4 and flag3
end

sqr16.cuffButton.Activated:Connect(function()
  if sqr16.selectedPlayer then
    f12(sqr16.selectedPlayer)
  end
end)

local v37

local function f13(selectedPlayer2)
  if sqr16.autoCuffTargetConnection then
    pcall(function() sqr16.autoCuffTargetConnection:Disconnect() end)
    sqr16.autoCuffTargetConnection = nil
  end

  v37 = selectedPlayer2

  if selectedPlayer2 and selectedPlayer2 ~= sqr16.player then
    sqr16.autoCuffTargetConnection = selectedPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
      sqr16.lastCuffTime = 0
    end)
  end
end

local function f14()
  if sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive then
    return
  end

  sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive = true

  sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop = task.spawn(function()
    while sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive and sqr16.autoCuffEnabled do
      local selectedPlayer3 = sqr16.selectedPlayer

      if not selectedPlayer3 or not selectedPlayer3.Parent then
        for _, value19 in ipairs((sqr16.Players:GetPlayers())) do
          if value19 ~= sqr16.player
            and (not sqr16.canTargetPlayer or sqr16.canTargetPlayer(value19))
            and value19.Character then
            selectedPlayer3 = value19
            sqr16.updateTargetUI(value19)
            break
          end
        end
      end

      if selectedPlayer3 ~= v37 then
        f13(selectedPlayer3)
      end

      if selectedPlayer3 and selectedPlayer3 ~= sqr16.player and selectedPlayer3.Parent then
        local character4 = selectedPlayer3.Character

        local humanoidRootPart12 = character4
        humanoidRootPart12 = character4 and character4:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local character5 = sqr16.player.Character

        local humanoidRootPart13 = character5
        humanoidRootPart13 = character5 and character5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart12 and humanoidRootPart13 and not sqr16.cuffInProgress then
          if (humanoidRootPart13.Position - humanoidRootPart12.Position).Magnitude > 18
            or tick() - (sqr16.lastCuffTime or 0) > 4 then
            if f12(selectedPlayer3) then
              sqr16.lastCuffTime = tick()
            end
          end
        end
      end

      task.wait(0.15)
    end

    sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop = nil
    sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive = false
  end)

  sqr16.autoCuffLoop = sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop
end

local function f15()
  sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive = false

  if sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop then
    pcall(function() task.cancel(sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop) end)
  end

  sqr16.autoCuffMonitorLoop = nil

  if sqr16.autoCuffLoop then
    pcall(function() task.cancel(sqr16.autoCuffLoop) end)
  end

  sqr16.autoCuffLoop = nil
end

if not sqr16.autoCuffSupervisorLoop then
  sqr16.autoCuffSupervisorLoop = task.spawn(function()
    while sqr16.remoteCommandAlive ~= false do
      task.wait(0.5)

      if sqr16.autoCuffEnabled and not sqr16.autoCuffMonitorAlive then
        pcall(f14)
      end

      if sqr16.autoCuffEnabled then
        local selectedPlayer4 = sqr16.selectedPlayer

        if selectedPlayer4 and selectedPlayer4 ~= v37 then
          pcall(f13, selectedPlayer4)
        end
      end
    end
  end)
end

sqr16.autoCuffButton.Activated:Connect(function()
  sqr16.autoCuffEnabled = not sqr16.autoCuffEnabled

  if sqr16.autoCuffEnabled then
    sqr16.autoCuffButton.Text = "إيقاف الكلبشه"
    sqr16.autoCuffButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
    sqr16.lastCuffTime = 0

    f14()
  else
    f15()
    sqr16.autoCuffButton.Text = "كلبشه تلقائيه"
    sqr16.autoCuffButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  end
end)

local sqr17 = _G.SQR

function sqr17.updateTargetUI(text9)
  if text9 then
    if sqr17.canTargetPlayer and not sqr17.canTargetPlayer(text9) then
      return
    end

    sqr17.selectedPlayer = text9
    sqr17.nameLabel.Text = text9.DisplayName
    sqr17.userLabel.Text = "@" .. text9.Name
    sqr17.targetAvatar.Visible = true

    pcall(function()
      local players2 = sqr17.Players

      sqr17.targetAvatar.Image = players2:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
        text9.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
      )
    end)
  else
    sqr17.selectedPlayer = nil
    sqr17.nameLabel.Text = "No player"
    sqr17.userLabel.Text = ""
    sqr17.targetAvatar.Image = ""
    sqr17.targetAvatar.Visible = false
  end
end

  frame4.BorderSizePixel = 0
  frame4.Parent = leavePopup

  local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

  imageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -25)
  imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
  imageLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0

  imageLabel.Image = sqr17.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
    text10.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
  )

  imageLabel.Parent = frame4

  local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
  textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.35, 0)
  textLabel2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0.05, 0)

  textLabel2.BorderSizePixel = 0

  textLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
  textLabel2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
  textLabel2.Parent = frame4

  local textLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
  textLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.35, 0)
  textLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0.4, 0)

  textLabel3.BorderSizePixel = 0
  textLabel3.Text = "Left " .. text11 .. " times"
  textLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
  textLabel3.TextScaled = true
  textLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
  textLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
  textLabel3.Parent = frame4

  frame4.Position = UDim2.new(0.01, -200, 0.15, 0)

  sqr17.TweenService:Create(frame4, TweenInfo.new(
    0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
  ), { Position = UDim2.new(0.01, 10, 0.15, 0) }):Play()

  delay(5, function()
    sqr17.TweenService:Create(frame4, TweenInfo.new(
      0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In
    ), { Position = UDim2.new(0.01, -200, 0.15, 0) }):Play()

    delay(0.3, function()
      if sqr17.leavePopup == leavePopup then
        leavePopup:Destroy()
        sqr17.leavePopup = nil
      end
    end)
  end)
end

function sqr17.ResetTargetActions()
  if sqr17.autoCuffEnabled then
    sqr17.autoCuffEnabled = false

    if sqr17.autoCuffLoop then
      pcall(function() task.cancel(sqr17.autoCuffLoop) end)
    end

    sqr17.autoCuffLoop = nil

    if sqr17.autoCuffButton and sqr17.autoCuffButton.Parent then
      sqr17.autoCuffButton.Text = "كلبشه تلقائيه"
      sqr17.autoCuffButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
    end
  end

  if sqr17.isRe then
    sqr17.isRe = false

    if sqr17.reLoop then
      sqr17.safeCancel(sqr17.reLoop)
    end

    sqr17.reButton.Text = "Re"
    sqr17.reButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  end

  if sqr17.isCopy1 then
    sqr17.isCopy1 = false

    if sqr17.copy1Loop then
      sqr17.safeCancel(sqr17.copy1Loop)
    end

    sqr17.copy1Button.Text = "C1"
    sqr17.copy1Button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  end

  if sqr17.isCopy2 then
    sqr17.isCopy2 = false

    if sqr17.copy2Loop then
      sqr17.safeCancel(sqr17.copy2Loop)
    end

    sqr17.copy2Button.Text = "C2"
    sqr17.copy2Button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  end

  if sqr17.BangActive or sqr17.HeadBangActive then
    sqr17.StopBang()
    sqr17.bangButton.Text = "Bang"
    sqr17.bangButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
    sqr17.headbangButton.Text = "Head"
    sqr17.headbangButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  end

  if sqr17.isViewing then
    sqr17.isViewing = false
    sqr17.viewButton.Text = "View"
    sqr17.viewButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)

    pcall(function() sqr17.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = sqr17.player.Character end)
  end
end

local sqr18 = _G.SQR

local function f16(text12)
  local gsub = tostring(text12 or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
  local list4 = {}

  if gsub == "" then
    return list4
  end

  for _, value21 in ipairs(sqr18.Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if value21 ~= sqr18.player and (not sqr18.canTargetPlayer or sqr18.canTargetPlayer(value21)) then
      local lower2 = tostring(value21.Name or ""):lower()
      local text13 = tostring(value21.DisplayName or "")
      local v38 = lower2 == gsub
      local lower3 = text13:lower()

      if v38 or lower3 == gsub or lower2:find(gsub, 1, true) or lower3:find(gsub, 1, true) then
        table.insert(list4, value21)
      end
    end
  end

  return list4
end

sqr18.findTargetMatches = f16

local function f17()
  local v39 = f16(sqr18.searchBox.Text)

  if v39[1] then
    sqr18.updateTargetUI(v39[1])
  elseif tostring(sqr18.searchBox.Text or ""):gsub("%s+", "") == "" then
    sqr18.updateTargetUI(nil)
  else
    sqr18.updateTargetUI(nil)
  end

  return v39
end

sqr18.searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(f17)
sqr18.searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() f17() end)

sqr18.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
  task.spawn(function()
    if sqr18.searchBox and tostring(sqr18.searchBox.Text or "") ~= "" then
      f17()
    end
  end)
end)

sqr18.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
  if player == sqr18.selectedPlayer then
    if not sqr18.playerLeaveData[player] then
      sqr18.playerLeaveData[player] = 0
    end

    sqr18.playerLeaveData[player] = sqr18.playerLeaveData[player] + 1

    sqr18.leftPlayersData[player] = {
      shouldReselect = true,
      leaveCount = sqr18.playerLeaveData[player],
    }

    sqr18.ShowLeavePopup(player, sqr18.playerLeaveData[player])
    sqr18.ResetTargetActions()
    sqr18.updateTargetUI(nil)
  end
end)

sqr18.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player2)
  if sqr18.leftPlayersData[player2] and sqr18.leftPlayersData[player2].shouldReselect then
    wait(0.5)

    if not sqr18.selectedPlayer then
      sqr18.updateTargetUI(player2)
    end

    sqr18.leftPlayersData[player2] = nil
  end
end)

local f18

local function makeTextBox(p36)
  local textBox2 = Instance.new("TextBox")
  textBox2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 35)
  textBox2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  textBox2.BorderSizePixel = 0
  textBox2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
  textBox2.PlaceholderText = "Message " .. tostring(#sqr20.hdSpamBoxes + 1)
  textBox2.Text = ""
  textBox2.ClearTextOnFocus = false
  textBox2.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
  textBox2.TextSize = 15
  textBox2.ZIndex = 6
  textBox2.Active = true
  textBox2.ZIndex = 60
  textBox2.Visible = true
  textBox2.Parent = sqr20.hdSpamList

  Instance.new("UICorner", textBox2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
  table.insert(sqr20.hdSpamBoxes, textBox2)

  if not p36 then
    f18()
  end

  return textBox2
end

function f18()
  task.defer(function()
    if sqr20.hdSpamList and sqr20.hdSpamLayout then
      sqr20.hdSpamList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
        0, 0, 0, sqr20.hdSpamLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 20
      )
    end
  end)
end

local function f19(p37)
  local removed3 = table.remove(sqr20.hdSpamBoxes)

  if removed3 then
    removed3:Destroy()
  end

  if not p37 then
    f18()
  end
end

local function f20(p38)
  local clamped8 = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(p38) or 1), 1, 1000)

  while #sqr20.hdSpamBoxes < clamped8 do
    makeTextBox(true)
  end

  while #sqr20.hdSpamBoxes > clamped8 do
    f19(true)
  end

  sqr20.hdCountBox.Text = tostring(clamped8)
  f18()
end

makeTextBox()

sqr20.hdCountBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
  local v46 = tonumber(sqr20.hdCountBox.Text)

  if not v46 then
    sqr20.hdCountBox.Text = tostring(#sqr20.hdSpamBoxes)
    return

            table.insert(list5, v51)
          end

          break
        elseif v49 > #list5 then
          v49 = 1
        end
      end

local sqr22 = _G.SQR

function sqr22.sectionAllowed(p39)
  if p39 == "VIP" then
    return sqr22.isVIP or sqr22.isOwner
  end

  if p39 == "Owner" then
    return sqr22.isOwner
  end

  return true
end

function sqr22.refreshSectionVisibility()
  if not sqr22.sectionAllowed(sqr22.sections[sqr22.currentSection] or "Setting") then
    sqr22.currentSection = 1
  end

  local count = 0

  for index5, value27 in ipairs(sqr22.sectionFrames) do
    local v53 = sqr22.sectionAllowed(sqr22.sections[index5])
    local v54 = v53 and index5 == sqr22.currentSection

    if value27 then
      value27.Visible = v54
    end

    local v55 = sqr22.sectionButtons[index5]

    if v55 then
      v55.Visible = v53
      v55.Active = v53

      if v53 then
        count = count + 1
        v55.Position = UDim2.new(0.06, 0, 0.02 + (count - 1) * 0.082, 0)
      end

      v55.BackgroundColor3 = v54 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 45)
      v55.BackgroundTransparency = v54 and 0 or 0.4
      v55.TextColor3 = v54 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
      v55.TextTransparency = 0
      v55.ZIndex = 5
    end
  end
end

function sqr22.switchSectionByName(p40)
  for index6, value28 in ipairs(sqr22.sections) do
    if value28 == p40 then
      if not sqr22.sectionAllowed(value28) then
        return false
      end

      sqr22.currentSection = index6
      sqr22.refreshSectionVisibility()

      return true
    end
  end

  return false
end

local sqr23 = _G.SQR
local players3 = sqr23.Players
local rs = sqr23.rs
local coreGui2 = sqr23.CoreGui

sqr23.antiPlayerActive = false

sqr23.antiChatConnections = sqr23.antiChatConnections or {}

local function f21(p41)
  for k = #p41, 1, -1 do
    pcall(function() p41[k]:Disconnect() end)
    p41[k] = nil
  end
end

local function f22(character6)
  if not character6 then
    return
  end

  for _, value29 in ipairs(character6:GetDescendants()) do
    pcall(function()
      if value29:IsA("BasePart") then
        value29.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
        value29.CastShadow = true
      elseif value29:IsA("Decal") or value29:IsA("Texture") then
        value29.Transparency = 0
      elseif value29:IsA("BillboardGui") or value29:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
        value29.Enabled = true
      elseif value29:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or value29:IsA("Trail") or value29:IsA("Beam")
        or value29:IsA("Smoke") or value29:IsA("Fire") or value29:IsA("Sparkles")
        or value29:IsA("Highlight") then
        value29.Enabled = true
      end
    end)
  end

  pcall(function()
    local humanoid4 = character6:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if humanoid4 then
      humanoid4.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.Viewer
    end
  end)
end

local function f23(instance5)
  if not sqr23.antiPlayerActive or not instance5 then
    return
  end

  pcall(function()
    local humanoid5 = instance5:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if humanoid5 then
      humanoid5.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
      humanoid5.DisplayName = ""
    end
  end)

  for _, value30 in ipairs(instance5:GetDescendants()) do
    pcall(function()
      if value30:IsA("BasePart") then
        value30.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        value30.CastShadow = false
      elseif value30:IsA("Decal") or value30:IsA("Texture") then
        value30.Transparency = 1
      elseif value30:IsA("BillboardGui") or value30:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
        value30.Enabled = false
      elseif value30:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or value30:IsA("Trail") or value30:IsA("Beam")
        or value30:IsA("Smoke") or value30:IsA("Fire") or value30:IsA("Sparkles")
        or value30:IsA("Highlight") then
        value30.Enabled = false
      end
    end)
  end
end

local f24

local function f25(antiPlayerActive)
  sqr23.antiPlayerActive = antiPlayerActive
  f21(sqr23.antiPlayerConnections)

  if antiPlayerActive then
    for _, value31 in ipairs(players3:GetPlayers()) do
      if value31 ~= player3 then
        f24(value31)
      end
    end

    table.insert(sqr23.antiPlayerConnections, players3.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player4)
      if player4 ~= player3 and sqr23.antiPlayerActive then
        f24(player4)
      end
    end))
  else
    for _, value32 in ipairs(players3:GetPlayers()) do
      if value32 ~= player3 and value32.Character then
        f22(value32.Character)
      end
    end
  end
end

function f24(p42)
  if p42 == player3 then
    return
  end

  local function f26(character7)
    if not character7 then
      return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
      for m = 1, 8 do
        if not sqr23.antiPlayerActive or not character7.Parent then
          break
        end

        f23(character7)
        task.wait(0.15)
      end
    end)

    table.insert(sqr23.antiPlayerConnections, character7.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant2)
      if not sqr23.antiPlayerActive then
        return
      end

      pcall(function()
        if descendant2:IsA("BasePart") then
          descendant2.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
          descendant2.CastShadow = false
        elseif descendant2:IsA("Decal") or descendant2:IsA("Texture") then
          descendant2.Transparency = 1
        elseif descendant2:IsA("BillboardGui") or descendant2:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
          descendant2.Enabled = false
        elseif descendant2:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant2:IsA("Trail")
          or descendant2:IsA("Beam") or descendant2:IsA("Smoke") or descendant2:IsA("Fire")
          or descendant2:IsA("Sparkles") or descendant2:IsA("Highlight") then
          descendant2.Enabled = false
        end
      end)
    end))
  end

  local characterAdded = p42.CharacterAdded
  table.insert(sqr23.antiPlayerConnections, characterAdded:Connect(f26))

  if p42.Character then
    f26(p42.Character)
  end
end

local list6 = {
  "chat", "chatframe", "chatwindow", "chatbox", "chatbar", "chatcontainer", "chatgui",
  "bubblechat", "bubble", "messaging", "textchat",
}

local function f27(descendant3, p43)
  pcall(function()
    if descendant3:IsA("ScreenGui") or descendant3:IsA("BillboardGui")
      or descendant3:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
      descendant3.Enabled = not p43
    elseif descendant3:IsA("GuiObject") then
      descendant3.Visible = not p43
    end
  end)
end

local function f28(descendant4)
  local parent5 = descendant4

  for n = 1, 8 do
    if not parent5 then
      break
    end

    local lower4 = tostring(parent5.Name or ""):lower()

    if lower4 == "sqr_ui" or lower4 == "protectioncenterui" or lower4:find("cmdbar", 1, true) then
      return true
    end

    parent5 = parent5.Parent
  end

  return false
end

local function f29(name11)
  local lower5 = tostring(name11 or ""):lower()

  for _, value33 in ipairs(list6) do
    if lower5 == value33 or lower5:find(value33, 1, true) then
      return true
    end
  end

  return false
end

local function f30(instance6, p44)
  if not instance6 then
    return
  end

  for _, value34 in ipairs(instance6:GetDescendants()) do
    if not f28(value34) and f29(value34.Name) then
      f27(value34, p44)
    end
  end

  for _, value35 in ipairs(instance6:GetChildren()) do
    if not f28(value35) and f29(value35.Name) then
      f27(value35, p44)
    end
  end
end

local function f31(descendant5)
  pcall(function()
    if f28(descendant5) then
      return
    end

    if not (descendant5:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant5:IsA("TextButton")
      or descendant5:IsA("TextBox")) then
      return
    end

    local lower6 = tostring(descendant5.Text or ""):lower()

    if not (lower6:find("message from foreverhd", 1, true)
      or lower6:find("message from owner", 1, true)
      or lower6:find("message from", 1, true)
        and (lower6:find("foreverhd", 1, true) or lower6:find("owner", 1, true))
      or lower6:find("settings prevent you from sending", 1, true)
      or lower6:find("your chat settings prevent you from sending", 1, true)) then
      return
    end

    local parent6 = descendant5
    local instance7

    for i6 = 1, 12 do
      parent6 = parent6.Parent

      if not parent6 then
        break
      end

      local lower7 = tostring(parent6.Name or ""):lower()

      if lower7 == "sqr_ui" or lower7 == "protectioncenterui" or lower7:find("cmdbar", 1, true) then
        break
      end

      if parent6:IsA("ScreenGui") or parent6:IsA("CoreGui") then
        break
      elseif parent6:IsA("GuiObject") then
        instance7 = parent6
      end
    end

    if instance7 and instance7.Parent then
      instance7.Visible = false
    else
      descendant5.Visible = false
    end

    descendant5.Visible = false
  end)
end

local function f32(p45)
  sqr23.antiChatActive = p45

  pcall(function() TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration.Enabled = not p45 end)
  pcall(function() TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.Enabled = not p45 end)
  pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, not p45) end)

  f30(playerGui2, p45)
  f30(coreGui2, p45)

  for _, value36 in ipairs({ playerGui2, coreGui2 }) do
    if value36 then
      for _, value37 in ipairs(value36:GetDescendants()) do
        f31(value37)
      end
    end
  end
end

local function f33()
  f21(sqr23.antiChatConnections)

  table.insert(sqr23.antiChatConnections, playerGui2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant6)
    f31(descendant6)

    if not f28(descendant6) and sqr23.antiChatActive and f29(descendant6.Name) then
      f27(descendant6, true)
    end
  end))

  table.insert(sqr23.antiChatConnections, coreGui2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant7)
    f31(descendant7)

    if not f28(descendant7) and sqr23.antiChatActive and f29(descendant7.Name) then
      f27(descendant7, true)
    end
  end))
end

f33()

pcall(function()

local function getExecuteClientCommand()
  local hdAdminHDClient2 = rs:FindFirstChild("HDAdminHDClient")
  local signals2 = hdAdminHDClient2 and hdAdminHDClient2:FindFirstChild("Signals")
  local executeClientCommand = signals2 and signals2:FindFirstChild("ExecuteClientCommand")

  if not executeClientCommand then
    pcall(function() executeClientCommand = rs:FindFirstChild("ExecuteClientCommand", true) end)
  end

  if executeClientCommand then
    sqr23.hdFolder = hdAdminHDClient2 or executeClientCommand.Parent
    sqr23.hdSignals = signals2 or executeClientCommand.Parent
    sqr23.executeCmd = executeClientCommand

    return executeClientCommand
  end

  return nil
end

task.spawn(function()
  for i7 = 1, 60 do
    if getExecuteClientCommand() then
      break
    end

    task.wait(0.25)
  end
end)

local function f34(p46)
  local executeClientCommand2 = getExecuteClientCommand()

  if not executeClientCommand2 or typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then
    return false
  end

  return (pcall(function()
    for _, value38 in ipairs((getconnections(executeClientCommand2.OnClientEvent))) do
      pcall(function()
        if p46 then
          if value38.Disable then
            value38:Disable()
          end
        elseif value38.Enable then
          value38:Enable()
        end
      end)
    end
  end))
end

local function f35()
  if _G.SQR_NV_FIRE_HOOK_INSTALLED then
    return
  end

  if not sqr23.executeCmd or typeof(firesignal) ~= "function"
    or typeof(hookfunction) ~= "function" then
    return
  end

  local v56 = firesignal
  local flag5 = false

  local function f36()
    hookfunction(firesignal, function(p47, ...)
      local sqr24 = _G.SQR

      if sqr24 and p47 == sqr24.executeCmd then
        local v57 = { ... }
        local v58 = #v57
        local v59 = {}

        for i8 = 1, v58 do
          if type(v57[i8]) == "string" then
            local text17 = v57[i8]
            v59[text17:lower()] = true
          end
        end

        if sqr24.nvActive and (v59.nightvision or v59.nv) then
          return nil
        end

        if sqr24.warpActive and v59.warp then
          return nil
        end
      end

      return v56(p47, ...)
    end)

    flag5 = true
  end

  if pcall(f36) and flag5 then
    _G.SQR_NV_FIRE_HOOK_INSTALLED = true
    _G.SQR_NV_OLD_FIRESIGNAL = v56
  end
end

task.spawn(function()

  while task.wait(1.5) do
    local executeClientCommand3 = getExecuteClientCommand()

    if executeClientCommand3 ~= v60 then
      v60 = executeClientCommand3

      if executeClientCommand3 then
        f35()

        if sqr23.nvActive or sqr23.warpActive then
          f34(true)
        end
      end
    end
  end
end)

  local function f37()
    pcall(function()
      if v61.skyConnection then
        v61.skyConnection:Disconnect()
        v61.skyConnection = nil
      end

      for key6, value39 in pairs(v61.parts) do
        if key6 and key6.Parent then
          key6.LocalTransparencyModifier = value39.transparency
          key6.CanCollide = value39.canCollide
          key6.CanTouch = value39.canTouch
          key6.CanQuery = value39.canQuery

          if value39.castShadow ~= nil then
            key6.CastShadow = value39.castShadow
          end
        end
      end

      for key7, value40 in pairs(v61.decals) do
        if key7 and key7.Parent then
          key7.Transparency = value40
        end
      end

      for key8, value41 in pairs(v61.effects) do
        if key8 and key8.Parent then
          key8.Enabled = value41
        end
      end

      for key9, value42 in pairs(v61.postEffects) do
        if key9 and key9.Parent then
          key9.Enabled = value42
        end
      end

      for key10, value43 in pairs(v61.atmospheres) do
        if key10 and key10.Parent then
          key10.Density = value43
        end
      end

      for key11, value44 in pairs(v61.clouds) do
        if key11 and key11.Parent then
          key11.Enabled = value44
        end
      end

      for key12, value45 in pairs(v61.workspaceClouds) do
        if key12 and key12.Parent then
          key12.Enabled = value45
        end
      end

      for key13, value46 in pairs(v61.skies) do
        if key13 and key13.Parent == nil then
          key13.Parent = value46
        end
      end

      if v61.floorConnection then
        v61.floorConnection:Disconnect()
        v61.floorConnection = nil
      end

      if v61.fakeFloor then
        v61.fakeFloor:Destroy()
        v61.fakeFloor = nil
      end

      if v61.globalShadows ~= nil then
        lighting.GlobalShadows = v61.globalShadows
      end

      if v61.environmentDiffuseScale ~= nil then
        lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = v61.environmentDiffuseScale
      end

      if v61.environmentSpecularScale ~= nil then
        lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = v61.environmentSpecularScale
      end

      if v61.shadowSoftness ~= nil then
        lighting.ShadowSoftness = v61.shadowSoftness
      end

      if v61.lightingTechnology ~= nil then
        pcall(function() lighting.Technology = v61.lightingTechnology end)

      if v61.qualityLevel ~= nil then
        pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = v61.qualityLevel end)

  local function makePart()
    if v61.applied then
      return
    end

    v61.applied = true
    local character8 = sqr23.player.Character

    pcall(function()
      for _, value47 in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if not (character8 and value47:IsDescendantOf(character8)) then
          if value47:IsA("BasePart") then
            v61.parts[value47] = {
              transparency = value47.LocalTransparencyModifier,
              canCollide = value47.CanCollide,
              canTouch = value47.CanTouch,
              canQuery = value47.CanQuery,
              castShadow = value47.CastShadow,
            }

            value47.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            value47.CanCollide = false
            value47.CanTouch = false
            value47.CanQuery = false

            pcall(function() value47.CastShadow = false end)
          elseif value47:IsA("Decal") or value47:IsA("Texture") then
            v61.decals[value47] = value47.Transparency
            value47.Transparency = 1
          elseif value47:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or value47:IsA("Trail") or value47:IsA("Beam")
            or value47:IsA("Smoke") or value47:IsA("Fire") or value47:IsA("Sparkles") then
            v61.effects[value47] = value47.Enabled
            value47.Enabled = false
          elseif value47:IsA("Clouds") then
            v61.workspaceClouds[value47] = value47.Enabled
            value47.Enabled = false
          end
        end
      end
    end)

    pcall(function()
      for _, value48 in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if value48:IsA("PostEffect") then
          v61.postEffects[value48] = value48.Enabled
          value48.Enabled = false
        elseif value48:IsA("Atmosphere") then
          v61.atmospheres[value48] = value48.Density
          value48.Density = 0
        elseif value48:IsA("Clouds") then
          v61.clouds[value48] = value48.Enabled
          value48.Enabled = false
        elseif value48:IsA("Sky") then
          v61.skies[value48] = value48.Parent
          value48.Parent = nil
        end
      end

      v61.globalShadows = lighting.GlobalShadows
      v61.environmentDiffuseScale = lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
      v61.environmentSpecularScale = lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
      v61.shadowSoftness = lighting.ShadowSoftness

      pcall(function() v61.lightingTechnology = lighting.Technology end)

      lighting.GlobalShadows = false
      lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
      lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
      lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0

      pcall(function() lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Compatibility end)
    end)

    pcall(function()
      local rendering = settings().Rendering
      v61.qualityLevel = rendering.QualityLevel
      rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
    end)

    pcall(function()
      local terrain2 = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

      if terrain2 then
        v61.terrainDecoration = terrain2.Decoration
        v61.waterWaveSize = terrain2.WaterWaveSize
        v61.waterWaveSpeed = terrain2.WaterWaveSpeed
        v61.waterReflectance = terrain2.WaterReflectance
        v61.waterTransparency = terrain2.WaterTransparency

        terrain2.Decoration = false
        terrain2.WaterWaveSize = 0
        terrain2.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
        terrain2.WaterReflectance = 0
        terrain2.WaterTransparency = 1
      end
    end)

    pcall(function()
      local antiLagLocalFloor = Instance.new("Part")
      antiLagLocalFloor.Name = "AntiLag_LocalFloor"
      antiLagLocalFloor.Anchored = true
      antiLagLocalFloor.CanCollide = true
      antiLagLocalFloor.CanTouch = false
      antiLagLocalFloor.CanQuery = true
      antiLagLocalFloor.Size = Vector3.new(80, 1, 80)
      antiLagLocalFloor.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
      antiLagLocalFloor.Transparency = 0
      antiLagLocalFloor.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
      antiLagLocalFloor.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
      antiLagLocalFloor.Parent = workspace

      v61.fakeFloor = antiLagLocalFloor

      local function f38()
        if not flag6 or not antiLagLocalFloor.Parent then
          return
        end

        local character9 = sqr23.player.Character
        local humanoidRootPart16 = character9 and character9:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart16 then
          antiLagLocalFloor.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            humanoidRootPart16.Position.X, humanoidRootPart16.Position.Y - 3.2,
            humanoidRootPart16.Position.Z
          )
        end
      end

      f38()

      v61.floorConnection = sqr23.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(f38)
    end)
  end

  local function f39()
    if flag7 then
      return
    end

    flag7 = true

    task.spawn(function()
      makePart()

      while flag6 do
        task.wait(1)
      end

      f37()
      flag7 = false
    end)
  end

  local textButton10 = Instance.new("TextButton")
  textButton10.Parent = frame6
  textButton10.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 40)
  textButton10.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0, 146)

  textButton11.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 40)
  textButton11.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0, 250)

  local v63 = 0

  sqr23.fpsConnection = sqr23.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
    v62 = v62 + 1
    v63 = v63 + delta

    if v63 >= 0.5 then

      or Color3.fromRGB(178, 34, 34)

    else

    end
  end)

  local v64 = false

  local function f40(instance8)
    if not sqr23.systemCleanerProtectionActive then
      return
    end

    pcall(function()
      if string.lower(instance8.Name):find("system", 1, true) then
        instance8:Destroy()
        return
      end

      if instance8:IsA("TextLabel") or instance8:IsA("TextButton") then
        if string.lower(instance8.Text or ""):find("system", 1, true) then
          local parent7 = instance8.Parent

          if parent7 and parent7 ~= playerGui2 then
            parent7:Destroy()
          else
            instance8:Destroy()
          end
        end
      end
    end)
  end

  local function f41(playerGui3)
    if not playerGui3 then
      return
    end

    for _, value49 in ipairs(playerGui3:GetDescendants()) do
      f40(value49)
    end
  end

  local function f42()
    if not sqr23.systemCleanerProtectionActive then
      return
    end

    f41(playerGui2)
  end

  local function f43()
    if sqr23.protectionConnections.system then
      pcall(function() sqr23.protectionConnections.system:Disconnect() end)
      sqr23.protectionConnections.system = nil
    end

    if sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui then
      pcall(function() sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui:Disconnect() end)
      sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui = nil
    end

    if playerGui2 then
      local descendantAdded = playerGui2.DescendantAdded

      sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui = descendantAdded:Connect(function(p48)
        if sqr23.systemCleanerProtectionActive then
          task.defer(f40, p48)
        end
      end)
    end

    f42()
  end

  local function f44(p49)
    v64 = p49
    sqr23.systemCleanerProtectionActive = p49

    if p49 then
      f43()
    else
      if sqr23.protectionConnections.system then
        pcall(function() sqr23.protectionConnections.system:Disconnect() end)
        sqr23.protectionConnections.system = nil
      end

      if sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui then
        pcall(function() sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui:Disconnect() end)
        sqr23.protectionConnections.systemPlayerGui = nil
      end
    end
  end

  textButton10.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    f44(not v64)

    textButton10.BackgroundColor3 = v64 and Color3.fromRGB(46, 139, 87)
      or Color3.fromRGB(178, 34, 34)

  sqr23.addButtonFeedback(textButton11)

    f25(not sqr23.antiPlayerActive)

      or Color3.fromRGB(178, 34, 34)

      textButton7.Text = "AntiNv: ON"

      if sqr23.executeCmd then
        f34(true)
      end

    else
      textButton7.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(178, 34, 34)
      textButton7.Text = "AntiNv: OFF"

      if sqr23._protectionNvLoop then
        sqr23.safeCancel(sqr23._protectionNvLoop)
        sqr23._protectionNvLoop = nil
      end
    end
  end)

  textButton8.Activated:Connect(function()

    if sqr23.warpActive then
      textButton8.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 139, 87)
      textButton8.Text = "AntiWarp: ON"

      if sqr23.executeCmd then
        f34(true)
      end

      task.spawn(function() pcall(function() sqr23.sendCommand("!unwarp") end) end)
    else
      textButton8.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(178, 34, 34)
      textButton8.Text = "AntiWarp: OFF"

      if sqr23._protectionWarpLoop then
        sqr23.safeCancel(sqr23._protectionWarpLoop)
        sqr23._protectionWarpLoop = nil
      end
    end
  end)

  if protectionCenterUI2.Destroying then
    protectionCenterUI2.Destroying:Connect(function()
      if sysConn then
        pcall(function() sysConn:Disconnect() end)
      end
    end)
  end
end

local sqr25 = _G.SQR
local v65 = sqr25.sectionFrames[table.find(sqr25.sections, "Anti")]

sqr25.WifiOriginalPos = nil

function sqr25.clickButton(p50)
  if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
    if pcall(function() firesignal(p50.MouseButton1Click) end) then
      return true
    end
  end

  if typeof(getconnections) == "function" then
    local ok6, result5 = pcall(function() return getconnections(p50.MouseButton1Click) end)

    if ok6 and result5 then
      for _, value50 in ipairs(result5) do
        if value50.Function then
          pcall(function() value50.Function() end)
          return true
        end
      end
    end
  end

  return false
end

function sqr25.sendSkinCommand(p51)
end

local sqr26 = _G.SQR

function sqr26.createAntiButton(text18, p52, p53)
  local parent8 = sqr26.sectionFrames[table.find(sqr26.sections, "Anti")]

  local textButton13 = Instance.new("TextButton")
  textButton13.Size = UDim2.new(text18 == "AntiNv" and 0.9 or 0.45, 0, 0.09, 0)

  textButton13.Position = UDim2.new(
    text18 == "AntiNv" and 0.05 or p52, 0, text18 == "AntiNv" and 0.75 or p53, 0
  )

  textButton13.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
  textButton13.BorderSizePixel = 0
  textButton13.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
  textButton13.Text = text18 == "AntiNv" and "Anti-NV" or text18
  textButton13.TextScaled = true
  textButton13.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
  textButton13.Parent = parent8
  textButton13:SetAttribute("SQR_AntiName", text18)

  Instance.new("UICorner", textButton13).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
  local flag8 = false
  local v66

  local function f45()
    local remoteEvent2 = sqr26.rs:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent")

    if not remoteEvent2 or not remoteEvent2:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
      return
    end

      end

      table.clear(list7)

    local instance9

        if not flag8 then

        if type(map) == "table" then

              flag9 = true

          if lower9 == "aura" or lower9 == "dance" or lower9 == "emote" then
            flag9 = true

        if flag9 then

        end

  local function f51()
    v66 = task.spawn(function()
      while flag8 do
        local character11 = sqr26.player.Character

        if character11 and character11.Parent then
          local humanoid6 = character11:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
          local humanoidRootPart19 = character11:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

          if humanoid6 and humanoidRootPart19 and humanoidRootPart19.Parent then
            local cframe5 = humanoidRootPart19.CFrame
            local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local cframe6 = currentCamera and currentCamera.CFrame
            local cameraType = currentCamera and currentCamera.CameraType
            local cameraSubject = currentCamera and currentCamera.CameraSubject

            pcall(function()
              if currentCamera and cframe6 then
                currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
                currentCamera.CFrame = cframe6
              end

              humanoidRootPart19.CFrame = CFrame.new(999999, 999999, 999999)
              task.wait(0.05)

              if humanoidRootPart19.Parent then
                humanoidRootPart19.CFrame = cframe5
              end

              if currentCamera and cframe6 then
                currentCamera.CFrame = cframe6
                currentCamera.CameraType = cameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom

                if cameraSubject and cameraSubject.Parent then
                  currentCamera.CameraSubject = cameraSubject
                elseif humanoid6.Parent then
                  currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid6
                end
              end
            end)
          end
        end

        task.wait(0.6)
      end
    end)
  end

  local function f52()
    local function getHumanoidRootPart(player5)
      return player5 and player5.Character
        and player5.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local humanoidRootPart20 = getHumanoidRootPart(sqr26.player)

    if humanoidRootPart20 then
      sqr26.AntiBangOriginalPos = humanoidRootPart20.CFrame
    end

    local function f53(p54)
      local character12 = p54 and p54.Character

      if not character12 then
        return false
      end

      local humanoid7 = character12:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
      local animator2 = humanoid7 and humanoid7:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

      if animator2 then
        for _, value53 in ipairs(animator2:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
          local animation2 = value53.Animation

          if (animation2 and tostring(animation2.AnimationId) or ""):find("5918726674", 1, true) then
            return true
          end
        end
      end

      return false
    end

    v66 = spawn(function()
      while flag8 do
        pcall(function()
          local humanoidRootPart21 = getHumanoidRootPart(sqr26.player)

          if not humanoidRootPart21 then
            return
          end

          local list8 = {}

          if sqr26.selectedPlayer and sqr26.selectedPlayer ~= sqr26.player
            and sqr26.selectedPlayer.Parent then
            table.insert(list8, sqr26.selectedPlayer)
          else
            for _, value54 in ipairs(sqr26.Players:GetPlayers()) do
              if value54 ~= sqr26.player then
                table.insert(list8, value54)
              end
            end
          end

          for _, value55 in ipairs(list8) do
            local humanoidRootPart22 = getHumanoidRootPart(value55)

            if humanoidRootPart22 then
              if (humanoidRootPart21.Position - humanoidRootPart22.Position).Magnitude < 6
                and f53(value55) then
                if not sqr26.AntiBangOriginalPos then
                  sqr26.AntiBangOriginalPos = humanoidRootPart21.CFrame
                end

                for _, value56 in ipairs(humanoidRootPart21:GetChildren()) do
                  if value56:IsA("Attachment") or value56:IsA("AlignPosition")
                    or value56:IsA("AlignOrientation") then
                    pcall(function() value56:Destroy() end)
                  end
                end

                humanoidRootPart21.CFrame = sqr26.AntiBangOriginalPos + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
                humanoidRootPart21.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

                wait(0.08)

                if flag8 and humanoidRootPart21.Parent then
                  humanoidRootPart21.CFrame = sqr26.AntiBangOriginalPos
                  humanoidRootPart21.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end

                break
              end
            end
          end
        end)

        wait(0.03)
      end
    end)
  end

function sqr27.sendToNearPlayers(text19)
  local nearPlayers = sqr27.getNearPlayers()

  if #nearPlayers == 0 then
    return
  end

  for _, value60 in ipairs(nearPlayers) do
    local gsub2 = text19:gsub("user", value60.Name)
    sqr27.sendCommand(gsub2)
    wait(0.05)
  end
end

sqr27.nearSendButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  local text20 = sqr27.nearCmdBox.Text

  if text20 == "" then
    return
  end

  sqr27.sendToNearPlayers(text20)
  sqr27.nearStatus.Text = "Sent"
  sqr27.nearStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)

  wait(0.5)

  sqr27.nearStatus.Text = "Stopped"
  sqr27.nearStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
end)

sqr27.nearSpamButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  sqr27.nearSpamActive = not sqr27.nearSpamActive
  local text21, v74

  if sqr27.nearSpamActive then
    text21 = sqr27.nearCmdBox.Text

    if text21 == "" then
      sqr27.nearSpamActive = false
      return
    end

    v74 = tonumber(sqr27.nearSpeedBox.Text) or 0.5

    if v74 < 0.1 then
      v74 = 0.1
    end

    if v74 > 3 then
      v74 = 3
    end

    sqr27.nearSpamButton.Text = "Stop"
    sqr27.nearSpamButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
    sqr27.nearStatus.Text = "Running..."
    sqr27.nearStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)

    sqr27.nearSpamLoop = spawn(function()
      while sqr27.nearSpamActive do
        sqr27.sendToNearPlayers(text21)
        wait(v74)
      end
    end)
  else
    sqr27.nearSpamButton.Text = "Spam"
    sqr27.nearSpamButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
    sqr27.nearStatus.Text = "Stopped"
    sqr27.nearStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)

    if sqr27.nearSpamLoop then
      sqr27.safeCancel(sqr27.nearSpamLoop)
    end

    sqr27.nearSpamLoop = nil
  end
end)

    sqr27.nearReButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)
    sqr27.nearStatus.Text = "Stopped"
    sqr27.nearStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)

    if sqr27.nearReLoop then
      sqr27.safeCancel(sqr27.nearReLoop)
    end

    sqr27.nearReLoop = nil
  end
end)

    textLabel7.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

    local flag10 = true
    local flag11 = false
    local flag12 = false
    local position6, position7

    local function f60(input13)
      if not position6 or not position7 then
        return
      end

      local v77 = input13.Position - position6

      if math.abs(v77.X) > 5 or math.abs(v77.Y) > 5 then
        flag12 = true
      end

      local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

      local viewportSize = currentCamera2 and currentCamera2.ViewportSize
        or Vector2.new(800, 600)

      local absoluteSize = circleBtn.AbsoluteSize

      local clamped9 = math.clamp(
        position7.X.Offset + v77.X, 0, math.max(0, viewportSize.X - absoluteSize.X)
      )

      local clamped10 = math.clamp(
        position7.Y.Offset + v77.Y, 0, math.max(0, viewportSize.Y - absoluteSize.Y)
      )

      circleBtn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(clamped9, clamped10)
    end

    circleBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input14)
      if input14.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input14.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        flag11 = true
        flag12 = false
        position6 = input14.Position
        position7 = circleBtn.Position

        input14.Changed:Connect(function()
          if input14.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
            flag11 = false
          end
        end)
      end
    end)

    local inputChanged = circleBtn.InputChanged

    inputChanged:Connect(function(p61)

    pulseScale.Scale = 1

    task.spawn(function()
      while flag10 and sqr29.autoClickerAlive and circleBtn.Parent do

          local create3 = tweenService2:Create(pulseScale, TweenInfo.new(
            0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
          ), { Scale = 1.08 })

          create3:Play()
          create3.Completed:Wait()
        end) or not flag10 or not sqr29.autoClickerAlive or not circleBtn.Parent then
          break
        end

        if not pcall(function()
          local create4 = tweenService2:Create(pulseScale, TweenInfo.new(
            0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
          ), { Scale = 1 })

          create4:Play()
          create4.Completed:Wait()
        end) then
          break
        end

        pulseScale.Scale = 1
      end

      pcall(function() pulseScale.Scale = 1 end)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
      while flag10 and sqr29.autoClickerAlive and circleBtn.Parent do
        local v79 = circleBtn.AbsolutePosition + circleBtn.AbsoluteSize / 2

        pcall(function()
          vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(v79.X, v79.Y + 36, 0, true, game, 0)
          task.wait(0.03)
          vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(v79.X, v79.Y + 36, 0, false, game, 0)
        end)

        task.wait(1)
      end
    end)

    circleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
      if flag12 then
        flag12 = false
        return
      end

      if not flag10 then
        return
      end

      flag10 = false
      sqr29.autoClickerActive = false

  sqr29.lowServerBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    pcall(function()
      local url2 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. tostring(game.PlaceId)
        .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

      local ok10, result9 = pcall(function() return game:HttpGetAsync(url2) end)
      local body

      if ok10 then
        body = result9
      elseif typeof(request) == "function" then
        local v80 = request({ Url = url2, Method = "GET" })

        if v80 and v80.Body then
          body = v80.Body
        end
      elseif typeof(http_request) == "function" then
        local v81 = http_request({ Url = url2, Method = "GET" })

        if v81 and v81.Body then
          body = v81.Body
        end
      end

      if not body then
        return
      end

      local jsonDecode = sqr29.HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
      local huge = math.huge
      local id

      for _, value62 in ipairs(jsonDecode.data or {}) do
        if value62.id ~= game.JobId and value62.playing < value62.maxPlayers
          and value62.playing < huge then
          huge = value62.playing
          id = value62.id
        end
      end

      if id then
        teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, id, sqr29.player)
      end
    end)
  end)

  function sqr29.saveLastBackground(p63, p64)
    local gsub3 = tostring(p64 or ""):gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("rbxassetid://", "")

    if gsub3 == "" or not tonumber(gsub3) then
      return false
    end

    local v82 = { background = gsub3, backgroundName = tostring(p63 or "Custom") }
    local ok11, result10 = pcall(function() return sqr29.HttpService:JSONEncode(v82) end)

    if not ok11 or not result10 then
      return false
    end

    if type(writefile) == "function" then
      if pcall(function() writefile(sqr29.BACKGROUND_FILE, result10) end) then
        return true
      end
    end

    if type(savefile) == "function" then
      if pcall(function() savefile(sqr29.BACKGROUND_FILE, result10) end) then
        return true
      end
    end

    return false
  end

  function sqr29.loadLastBackground()
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then
      return nil, nil
    end

    return nil, nil
  end

  function sqr29.applyBackground(p65, p66)
    local gsub4 = tostring(p65 or ""):gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("rbxassetid://", "")

    if gsub4 == "" or not tonumber(gsub4) then
      return false
    end

    local v83 = tostring(p66 or "Custom")

    sqr29.settingState.background = gsub4
    sqr29.settingState.backgroundId = gsub4
    sqr29.settingState.backgroundName = v83
    sqr29.selectedBgName = v83

    local image = "rbxassetid://" .. gsub4

    sqr29.selectedBgImage.Image = image
    sqr29.selectedBgImage.Visible = true
    sqr29.bgPreview.Image = image
    sqr29.bgButton.Text = "Background: " .. v83 .. "  ▼"
    sqr29.saveLastBackground(v83, gsub4)

    return true
  end

  function sqr29.applyUIScale(p67)
    local clamped11 = math.clamp(tonumber(p67) or 100, 70, 130)
    local v84 = (clamped11 - 70) / 60
    local v85 = clamped11 / 100
    sqr29.settingState.uiScale = v85

    if sqr29.uiScale then
      sqr29.uiScale.Scale = v85
    end

    if sqr29.mainFrame then
      sqr29.mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -300 * v85, 0.5, -200 * v85)
    end

    sqr29.uiScaleFill.Size = UDim2.new(v84, 0, 1, 0)
    sqr29.uiScaleKnob.Position = UDim2.new(v84, -9, 0.5, -9)
    sqr29.uiScaleValueLabel.Text = "Size: " .. math.floor(clamped11) .. "%"
  end

  function f58(p68)
    local x2 = sqr29.uiScaleSlider.AbsolutePosition.X
    local clamp = math.clamp
    local highest = math.max(sqr29.uiScaleSlider.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
    local v86 = clamp((p68 - x2) / highest, 0, 1)
    sqr29.applyUIScale(70 + v86 * 60)
  end

  v75 = false

  sqr29.uiScaleSlider.InputBegan:Connect(function(input16)
    if input16.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
      or input16.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
      v75 = true
      f58(input16.Position.X)
    end
  end)

  sqr29.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input17)
    if v75
      and (input17.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input17.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
      f58(input17.Position.X)
    end
  end)

  sqr29.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input18)

      or input18.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
      if v75 then
        v75 = false
        sqr29.saveSettings()
      end
    end
  end)

  function sqr29.clampBox(text24, p69)
    local v87 = tonumber(text24.Text) or p69
    local clamped12 = math.clamp(v87, 1, 1000)
    text24.Text = tostring(math.floor(clamped12))
    return clamped12
  end

  sqr29.speedBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()

    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

    sqr29.settingState.flySpeed = sqr29.clampBox(sqr29.flySpeedBox, 50)
    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

  sqr29.speedBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()

    if v88 then

      sqr29.saveSettings()
    end
  end)

  sqr29.flySpeedBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()

      sqr29.saveSettings()
    end
  end)

  function sqr29.getHumanoid()
    return sqr29.player.Character and sqr29.player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
  end

  function sqr29.applyWalkSpeed()
    local humanoid9 = sqr29.getHumanoid()

    if humanoid9 then
      humanoid9.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(sqr29.settingState.walkSpeed) or 16, 1, 1000)
    end
  end

  sqr29.freezeBtn.Activated:Connect(function()

    if not humanoidRootPart26 then
      return

    sqr29.settingState.frozen = not sqr29.settingState.frozen

      or Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
