  sqr29.flyConnection = nil
  sqr29.flyVelocity = nil
  sqr29.flyGyro = nil
  sqr29.flyHumanoid = nil
  sqr29.flyOldAutoRotate = nil
  sqr29.flyRespawnConnection = sqr29.flyRespawnConnection or nil

  function sqr29.stopFly()
    sqr29.settingState.flying = false

    if sqr29.flyConnection then
      sqr29.flyConnection:Disconnect()
      sqr29.flyConnection = nil
    end

    if sqr29.flyVelocity then
      sqr29.flyVelocity:Destroy()
      sqr29.flyVelocity = nil
    end

    if sqr29.flyGyro then
      sqr29.flyGyro:Destroy()
      sqr29.flyGyro = nil
    end

    if sqr29.flyHumanoid and sqr29.flyHumanoid.Parent then
      sqr29.flyHumanoid.AutoRotate = sqr29.flyOldAutoRotate == nil and true
        or sqr29.flyOldAutoRotate
    end

    sqr29.flyHumanoid = nil
    sqr29.flyOldAutoRotate = nil

    local humanoidRootPart27 = sqr29.player.Character
      and sqr29.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart27 then
      humanoidRootPart27.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    sqr29.flyBtn.Text = "Fly"
    sqr29.flyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  end

  function sqr29.startFly()
    local character13 = sqr29.player.Character
    local humanoidRootPart28 = character13 and character13:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid10 = character13 and character13:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not humanoidRootPart28 or not humanoid10 then
      return
    end

    sqr29.stopFly()
    sqr29.settingState.flying = true
    sqr29.flyHumanoid = humanoid10
    sqr29.flyOldAutoRotate = humanoid10.AutoRotate

    humanoid10.AutoRotate = false

    sqr29.flyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    sqr29.flyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(10000000, 10000000, 10000000)
    sqr29.flyVelocity.P = 10000
    sqr29.flyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    sqr29.flyVelocity.Parent = humanoidRootPart28
    sqr29.flyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    sqr29.flyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(10000000, 10000000, 10000000)
    sqr29.flyGyro.P = 10000
    sqr29.flyGyro.D = 500
    sqr29.flyGyro.CFrame = humanoidRootPart28.CFrame
    sqr29.flyGyro.Parent = humanoidRootPart28

    sqr29.flyConnection = sqr29.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
      if not sqr29.settingState.flying or not humanoidRootPart28.Parent or not humanoid10.Parent then
        sqr29.stopFly()
        return
      end

      local currentCamera3 = sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera

      if not currentCamera3 then
        return
      end

      local moveDirection = humanoid10.MoveDirection
      local lookVector3 = currentCamera3.CFrame.LookVector
      local rightVector = currentCamera3.CFrame.RightVector
      local vector3 = Vector3.new(lookVector3.X, 0, lookVector3.Z)
      local vector4 = Vector3.new(rightVector.X, 0, rightVector.Z)

      if vector3.Magnitude > 0 then
        vector3 = vector3.Unit
      end

      if vector4.Magnitude > 0 then
        vector4 = vector4.Unit
      end

      local zero = Vector3.zero

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
        zero = zero + vector3
      end

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
        zero = zero - vector3
      end

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
        zero = zero + vector4
      end

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
        zero = zero - vector4
      end

      if zero.Magnitude > 0 then
        moveDirection = zero
      end

      local v90 = 0

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or humanoid10.Jump then
        v90 = v90 + 1
      end

      if sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
        or sqr29.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
        v90 = v90 - 1
      end

      local vector5 = Vector3.new(moveDirection.X, v90, moveDirection.Z)
      local clamped13 = math.clamp(tonumber(sqr29.settingState.flySpeed) or 50, 1, 1000)

      sqr29.flyVelocity.Velocity = vector5.Magnitude > 0 and vector5.Unit * clamped13
        or Vector3.zero

      local vector6 = Vector3.new(lookVector3.X, 0, lookVector3.Z)

      if vector6.Magnitude > 0 then
        sqr29.flyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
          humanoidRootPart28.Position, humanoidRootPart28.Position + vector6.Unit
        )
      end
    end)

    sqr29.flyBtn.Text = "Unfly"
    sqr29.flyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 150, 90)
  end

  if not sqr29.settingRespawnConnection then
    sqr29.settingRespawnConnection = sqr29.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
      task.wait(0.15)
      pcall(function() sqr29.applyWalkSpeed() end)

      if sqr29.settingState.flying then
        pcall(function() sqr29.startFly() end)
      end
    end)
  end

  sqr29.flyBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    if sqr29.settingState.flying then
      sqr29.stopFly()
    else
      sqr29.startFly()
    end

    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

  sqr29.cameraConnection = nil
  sqr29.savedCameraCFrame = nil

  sqr29.cameraBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen = not sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen
    local currentCamera4 = sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen then
      if currentCamera4 then
        sqr29.savedCameraCFrame = currentCamera4.CFrame
      end

      if sqr29.cameraConnection then
        sqr29.cameraConnection:Disconnect()
      end

      sqr29.cameraConnection = sqr29.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen and sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera
          and sqr29.savedCameraCFrame then
          sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = sqr29.savedCameraCFrame
        end
      end)

      sqr29.cameraBtn.Text = "Camera Frozen"
      sqr29.cameraBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 60)
    else
      if sqr29.cameraConnection then
        sqr29.cameraConnection:Disconnect()
        sqr29.cameraConnection = nil
      end

      if sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera then
        local cameraSubject2 = sqr29.getHumanoid()

        if cameraSubject2 then
          sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = cameraSubject2
        end
      end

      sqr29.cameraBtn.Text = "Freeze Camera"
      sqr29.cameraBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
    end

    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

  function sqr29.setCharacterHidden(p70)
    if not sqr29.player.Character then
      return
    end

    for _, value64 in ipairs(sqr29.player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
      if value64:IsA("BasePart") or value64:IsA("Decal") or value64:IsA("Texture") then
        value64.LocalTransparencyModifier = p70 and 1 or 0
      elseif value64:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or value64:IsA("Trail") or value64:IsA("Beam")
        or value64:IsA("Smoke") or value64:IsA("Fire") or value64:IsA("Sparkles") then
        value64.Enabled = not p70
      end
    end
  end

  sqr29.hideBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    sqr29.settingState.hidden = not sqr29.settingState.hidden
    sqr29.setCharacterHidden(sqr29.settingState.hidden)
    sqr29.hideBtn.Text = sqr29.settingState.hidden and "Show Character" or "Hide Character"

    sqr29.hideBtn.BackgroundColor3 = sqr29.settingState.hidden and Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 60)
      or Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)

    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

  sqr29.respawnConnection = nil
  sqr29.respawnDiedConnection = nil
  sqr29.respawnRemovingConnection = nil
  sqr29.autoRespawnTask = nil

  function sqr29.forceAutoRespawn()
    if not sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn then
      return
    end

    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer

    if not localPlayer then
      return
    end

    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
      localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
      return
    end

    pcall(function() localPlayer:LoadCharacter() end)
  end

  function sqr29.bindAutoRespawnCharacter(instance10)
    task.wait(0.2)

    if not sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn then
      return
    end

    sqr29.applyWalkSpeed()

    if sqr29.settingState.hidden then
      sqr29.setCharacterHidden(true)
    end

    if sqr29.settingState.frozen then
      local humanoidRootPart29 = instance10 and instance10:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if humanoidRootPart29 then
        humanoidRootPart29.Anchored = true
      end
    end
  end

  function sqr29.startAutoRespawn()
    if sqr29.respawnConnection then
      return
    end

    sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn = true

    sqr29.respawnConnection = sqr29.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character14)
      sqr29.bindAutoRespawnCharacter(character14)
    end)

    sqr29.autoRespawnTask = task.spawn(function()
      while sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn do
        local localPlayer2 = players.LocalPlayer
        local character15 = localPlayer2 and localPlayer2.Character

        local humanoid11 = character15
        humanoid11 = character15 and character15:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

        local v91 = not character15
        local v92 = humanoid11

        if v91 or not v92 then
          task.wait(0.2)
        elseif v92.Health <= 0 then
          pcall(function() localPlayer2:LoadCharacter() end)
          task.wait(0.5)
        else
          task.wait(0.2)
        end
      end
    end)

    if sqr29.player.Character then
      sqr29.bindAutoRespawnCharacter(sqr29.player.Character)
    end
  end

  function sqr29.stopAutoRespawn()
    sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn = false

    if sqr29.respawnConnection then
      sqr29.respawnConnection:Disconnect()
      sqr29.respawnConnection = nil
    end

    if sqr29.autoRespawnTask then
      pcall(function() sqr29.safeCancel(sqr29.autoRespawnTask) end)
      sqr29.autoRespawnTask = nil
    end
  end

  sqr29.respawnBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
    if sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn then
      sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn = false
      sqr29.stopAutoRespawn()
      sqr29.respawnBtn.Text = "Auto Respawn: OFF"
      sqr29.respawnBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
    else
      sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn = true
      sqr29.startAutoRespawn()

      task.defer(function()
        if sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn then
          local localPlayer3 = players.LocalPlayer

          if localPlayer3 and localPlayer3.Character
            and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
            localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
          end
        end
      end)

      sqr29.respawnBtn.Text = "Auto Respawn: ON"
      sqr29.respawnBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 150, 90)
    end

    sqr29.saveSettings()
  end)

  sqr29.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character16)
    wait(0.25)
    sqr29.applyWalkSpeed()

    if sqr29.settingState.frozen then
      local humanoidRootPart30 = character16:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if humanoidRootPart30 then
        humanoidRootPart30.Anchored = true
      end
    end

    if sqr29.settingState.hidden then
      sqr29.setCharacterHidden(true)
    end
  end)

  sqr29.applyWalkSpeed()

  local background2 = sqr29.settingState.background or sqr29.settingState.backgroundId

  if background2 and tostring(background2) ~= "" then
    local gsub7 = tostring(background2):gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("rbxassetid://", "")

    sqr29.settingState.background = gsub7
    sqr29.settingState.backgroundId = gsub7

    local backgroundName2 = sqr29.settingState.backgroundName or "Custom"

    for _, value65 in ipairs(sqr29.settingsData) do
      if tostring(value65[2]) == tostring(gsub7) then
        backgroundName2 = value65[1]
        break
      end
    end

    local backgroundName3 = sqr29.settingState.backgroundName or backgroundName2

    sqr29.applyBackground(gsub7, backgroundName3)
    sqr29.assetIdBox.Text = tostring(gsub7)
  end

  if sqr29.selectedBgImage then
    sqr29.selectedBgImage.ImageTransparency = 0
  end

  sqr29.applyUIScale((tonumber(sqr29.settingState.uiScale) or 1) * 100)

  if sqr29.settingState.frozen then
    local character17 = sqr29.player.Character

    local humanoidRootPart31 = character17

    humanoidRootPart31 = character17
      and sqr29.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart31 then
      humanoidRootPart31.Anchored = true
    end

    sqr29.freezeBtn.Text = "Unfreeze"
    sqr29.freezeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 60)
  end

  if sqr29.settingState.hidden then
    sqr29.setCharacterHidden(true)
    sqr29.hideBtn.Text = "Show Character"
    sqr29.hideBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 60)
  end

  if sqr29.settingState.flying then
    task.defer(function()
      if sqr29.startFly then
        sqr29.startFly()
      end
    end)
  else
    sqr29.flyBtn.Text = "Fly"
    sqr29.flyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  end

  if sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen then
    local currentCamera5 = sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if currentCamera5 then
      sqr29.savedCameraCFrame = currentCamera5.CFrame
    end

    if sqr29.cameraConnection then
      sqr29.cameraConnection:Disconnect()
    end

    sqr29.cameraConnection = sqr29.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
      if sqr29.settingState.cameraFrozen and sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera
        and sqr29.savedCameraCFrame then
        sqr29.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = sqr29.savedCameraCFrame
      end
    end)

    sqr29.cameraBtn.Text = "Camera Frozen"
    sqr29.cameraBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 60)
  else
    sqr29.cameraBtn.Text = "Freeze Camera"
    sqr29.cameraBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  end

  if sqr29.settingState.autoRespawn then
    sqr29.startAutoRespawn()
    sqr29.respawnBtn.Text = "Auto Respawn: ON"
    sqr29.respawnBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 150, 90)
  else
    sqr29.respawnBtn.Text = "Auto Respawn: OFF"
    sqr29.respawnBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
  end

  sqr29.saveSettings()
end

  function f68(text26)
    if not text26 or not text26.row then
      return
    end

    local highest4 = text26.inServer
        and math.max(0, os.clock() - (text26.joinedAt or os.clock()))
      or 0

    local lowest = math.min(1440000, math.max(0, (text26.totalStay or 0) + highest4))

    if text26.stayLabel then
      text26.stayLabel.Text = "Stay :  " .. f67(lowest)
      text26.stayLabel.TextSize = 14

      text26.stayLabel.TextColor3 = math.floor(lowest) % 2 == 1
          and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    end

    if text26.leftLabel then
      text26.leftLabel.Text = "Left :  " .. tostring(text26.leftCount or 0)
      text26.leftLabel.TextSize = 14
    end

    if text26.statusLabel then
      text26.statusLabel.Text = text26.inServer and "Online" or "Offline"

      text26.statusLabel.TextColor3 = text26.inServer and Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 120)
        or Color3.fromRGB(220, 170, 80)

      text26.statusLabel.TextSize = 12
    end
  end

  sqr30.countLeaveQueue = sqr30.countLeaveQueue or {}

  function f64(p75)
    if not f61(p75) then
      return false
    end

    local v93 = f62(p75)
    local v94 = sqr30.countTracked[v93]

    if not v94 then
      v94 = {
        key = v93,
        userId = p75.UserId,
        name = p75.Name,
        displayName = p75.DisplayName,
        player = p75,
        totalStay = 0,
        joinedAt = os.clock(),
        leftCount = 0,
        inServer = true,
        order = #sqr30.countTracked + 1,
      }

      sqr30.countTracked[v93] = v94
    else
      if v94.inServer then
        v94.player = p75
        v94.name = p75.Name
        v94.displayName = p75.DisplayName

        if v94.row and v94.row.Parent then
          f68(v94)
        else
          f63(v94)
        end

        return true
      end

      v94.player = p75
      v94.name = p75.Name
      v94.displayName = p75.DisplayName
      v94.joinedAt = os.clock()
      v94.inServer = true
    end

    f63(v94)
    return true
  end

  function f65()
    for _, value66 in ipairs(sqr30.Players:GetPlayers()) do
      f64(value66)
    end
  end

  function f70(p77)
    local gsub8 = tostring(p77 or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")

    if gsub8 == "" then
      return {}
    end

    local list9 = {}

    for _, value67 in ipairs(sqr30.Players:GetPlayers()) do
      if value67 ~= sqr30.player
        and (value67.Name:lower() == gsub8 or value67.Name:lower():find(gsub8, 1, true)
          or value67.DisplayName and value67.DisplayName:lower():find(gsub8, 1, true)) then
        table.insert(list9, value67)
      end
    end

    return list9
  end

  sqr30.countTrackButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    local list10 = f70(sqr30.countSearchBox.Text)

    if #list10 == 0 then
      sqr30.countInfoLabel.Text = "لم يتم العثور على شخص يمكن تحديده"
      return
    end

    local count2 = 0

    for _, value68 in ipairs(list10) do
      if f64(value68) then
        count2 = count2 + 1
      end
    end

    sqr30.countInfoLabel.Text = "تم تحديد " .. tostring(count2) .. " شخص"
    sqr30.countSearchBox.Text = ""
  end)

  sqr30.countAllButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    sqr30.countAllEnabled = not sqr30.countAllEnabled

    if sqr30.countAllEnabled then
      sqr30.countAllButton.Text = "ALL ON"
      sqr30.countAllButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0)

      f65()
      sqr30.countInfoLabel.Text = "ALL ON — سيتم تحديد كل من يدخل تلقائياً"
    else
      sqr30.countAllButton.Text = "ALL"
      sqr30.countAllButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 55)
      sqr30.countInfoLabel.Text = "ALL OFF"
    end
  end)

  sqr30.countConnections = sqr30.countConnections or {}

  table.insert(sqr30.countConnections, sqr30.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player6)
    local v95 = f62(player6)
    local v96 = sqr30.countTracked[v95]

    if not v96 then
      return
    end

    if not v96.inServer then
      return
    end

    local max = math.max
    local lowest2 = math.min(1440000, max(0, os.clock() - (v96.joinedAt or os.clock())))

    v96.totalStay = math.min(1440000, (v96.totalStay or 0) + lowest2)
    v96.lastSessionDuration = lowest2
    v96.inServer = false
    v96.joinedAt = nil
    v96.leftCount = (v96.leftCount or 0) + 1
    v96.player = nil

    f68(v96)

    f69({
      userId = v96.userId,
      name = v96.name,
      sessionDuration = lowest2,
      times = v96.leftCount,
    })
  end))

  table.insert(sqr30.countConnections, sqr30.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player7)
    local v97 = f62(player7)
    local v98 = sqr30.countTracked[v97]

    if v98 then
      v98.player = player7
      v98.name = player7.Name
      v98.displayName = player7.DisplayName
      v98.inServer = true
      v98.joinedAt = os.clock()

      if v98.row and v98.row.Parent then
        f68(v98)
      else
        f63(v98)
      end
    elseif sqr30.countAllEnabled then
      f64(player7)
    end
  end))

  local heartbeat = sqr30.RunService.Heartbeat

  table.insert(sqr30.countConnections, heartbeat:Connect(function()
    for _, value69 in pairs(sqr30.countTracked) do
      f68(value69)
    end
  end))

  table.insert(sqr30.countConnections, sqr30.countLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
    sqr30.countScrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
      0, 0, 0, sqr30.countLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 20
    )
  end))

  task.defer(function()
    sqr30.countScrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
      0, 0, 0, sqr30.countLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 20
    )
  end)
end

local sqr31 = _G.SQR

  or ""

  or sqr31.SUPABASE_URL .. "/rest/v1/sqr_state"

local function f71(cloudRecord)
  local v99 = cloudRecord

  if type(v99) ~= "table" then
    v99 = {}
  end

  v99.activeUsers = type(v99.activeUsers) == "table" and v99.activeUsers or {}
  v99.owners = type(v99.owners) == "table" and v99.owners or {}
  v99.vips = type(v99.vips) == "table" and v99.vips or {}
  v99.bannedUsers = type(v99.bannedUsers) == "table" and v99.bannedUsers or {}
  v99.vipKicks = type(v99.vipKicks) == "table" and v99.vipKicks or {}
  v99.logs = type(v99.logs) == "table" and v99.logs or {}
  v99.commands = type(v99.commands) == "table" and v99.commands or {}
  v99.removedUsers = type(v99.removedUsers) == "table" and v99.removedUsers or {}
  v99.safeUsers = type(v99.safeUsers) == "table" and v99.safeUsers or {}
  v99.day = tostring(v99.day or os.date("!%Y-%m-%d"))
  v99.owners.nanMeWellDoit = true
  v99.owners.nanNeverLet = true

  if v99.day ~= os.date("!%Y-%m-%d") then
    v99.day = os.date("!%Y-%m-%d")
    v99.vipKicks = {}
  end

  return v99
end

local function f72(activeUsers, removedUsers)
  local v100 = {}
  local now2 = os.time()

  for key16, value70 in pairs(activeUsers or {}) do
    if type(value70) == "table"
      and now2 - (tonumber(value70.lastSeen) or 0) <= sqr31.SCRIPT_USER_TIMEOUT then
      local v101 = tostring(value70.userId or key16)

      if not (removedUsers and removedUsers[v101]) then
        value70.userId = tonumber(value70.userId) or tonumber(v101) or 0
        value70.name = tostring(value70.name or "Unknown")
        value70.displayName = tostring(value70.displayName or value70.name)
        value70.lastSeen = tonumber(value70.lastSeen) or now2

        v100[v101] = value70
      end
    end
  end

  return v100
end

local function f73()
  if typeof(request) == "function" then
    return request
  end

  if typeof(http_request) == "function" then
    return http_request
  end

  if typeof(syn) == "table" and typeof(syn.request) == "function" then
    return syn.request
  end

  return nil
end

local function f74(p78, jsonEncode3)
  local v102 = f73()

  if not v102 then
    return false, nil, "request function not found"
  end

  local v103 = p78 == "PUT" and "PATCH" or p78
  local supabaseSTATEURL = sqr31.SUPABASE_STATE_URL
  local url3

  if v103 == "GET" then
    url3 = supabaseSTATEURL .. "?id=eq.1&select=data"
  else
    url3 = supabaseSTATEURL .. "?id=eq.1"
  end

  local v104 = {
    Url = url3,
    Method = v103,
    Headers = {
      apikey = sqr31.SUPABASE_KEY,
      Authorization = "Bearer " .. sqr31.SUPABASE_KEY,
      ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
      Prefer = "return=representation",
    },
  }

  if v103 ~= "GET" then
    v104.Body = jsonEncode3
  end

  local ok13, result11 = pcall(function() return v102(v104) end)

  if not ok13 or not result11 then
    return false, nil, tostring(result11 or "request failed")
  end

  local v105 = tonumber(result11.StatusCode) or 0
  local v106 = tostring(result11.Body or "")

  if v105 < 200 or v105 >= 300 then
    return false, v106, "HTTP " .. tostring(v105) .. " " .. v106
  end

  return true, v106, nil
end

local function f75()
  local v107, v108, v109 = f74("GET")

  if not v107 then
    return nil, v109
  end

  local ok14, result12 = pcall(function() return sqr31.HttpService:JSONDecode(v108 or "") end)

  if not ok14 or type(result12) ~= "table" or not result12[1] then
    return {}, nil
  end

  local data2 = result12[1].data

  if type(data2) ~= "table" then
    return {}, nil
  end

  return data2, nil
end

local function f76(data3)
  if type(data3) ~= "table" then
    return false, "invalid record"
  end

  local v110 = {
    f74(
      "PUT",
      (sqr31.HttpService:JSONEncode({ data = data3, updated_at = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ") }))
    ),
  }

  return v110[1], v110[3]
end

local function f78(p79, name12)
  local v111 = f77(name12)

  for key17, value71 in pairs(p79 or {}) do
    if tostring(key17):lower() == v111 and value71 then
      return true
    end

    if type(value71) == "string" and value71:lower() == v111 then
      return true
    end
  end

  return false
end

function f77(p80)
  return tostring(p80 or ""):lower()
end

local function f79(p81)
  local v112 = f71(p81)

  sqr31.cloudRecord = v112

  sqr31.rankState = {
    owners = {},
    vips = {},
    bans = {},
    vipKicks = v112.vipKicks,
    day = v112.day,
  }

  sqr31.OWNER_LIST = {}
  sqr31.VIP_LIST = {}
  sqr31.BAN_LIST = {}

  for key18, value72 in pairs(v112.owners) do
    if value72 then
      sqr31.rankState.owners[f77(key18)] = true
      table.insert(sqr31.OWNER_LIST, tostring(key18))
    end
  end

  for _, value73 in ipairs({ "nanMeWellDoit", "nanNeverLet" }) do
    sqr31.rankState.owners[f77(value73)] = true
    local flag13 = false

    for _, value74 in ipairs(sqr31.OWNER_LIST) do
      if f77(value74) == f77(value73) then
        flag13 = true
        break
      end
    end

    if not flag13 then
      table.insert(sqr31.OWNER_LIST, value73)
    end
  end

  for key19, value75 in pairs(v112.vips) do
    if value75 and not f78(v112.owners, key19) then
      sqr31.rankState.vips[f77(key19)] = true
      table.insert(sqr31.VIP_LIST, tostring(key19))
    end
  end

  for key20, value76 in pairs(v112.bannedUsers) do
    if value76 then
      sqr31.rankState.bans[f77(key20)] = true
      table.insert(sqr31.BAN_LIST, tostring(key20))
    end
  end

  sqr31.isOwner = sqr31.rankState.owners[f77(sqr31.player.Name)] == true
  sqr31.isVIP = not sqr31.isOwner and sqr31.rankState.vips[f77(sqr31.player.Name)] == true
  sqr31.isBanned = sqr31.rankState.bans[f77(sqr31.player.Name)] == true
  sqr31.scriptUsers = v112.activeUsers

  return v112
end

function sqr31.isPlayerOwner(p82)
  return p82
      and (f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.owners, p82.Name) or p82.Name == "nanMeWellDoit"
        or p82.Name == "nanNeverLet")
    or false
end

function sqr31.isPlayerVIP(p83)
  return p83 and not sqr31.isPlayerOwner(p83) and f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.vips, p83.Name) or false
end

function sqr31.isPlayerBanned(p84)
  return p84 and f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.bannedUsers, p84.Name) or false
end

function sqr31.isPlayerSafe(p85)
  return p85 and f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.safeUsers, p85.Name) or false
end

function sqr31.getPlayerRank(p86)
  if sqr31.isPlayerOwner(p86) then
    return "Owner"
  end

  if sqr31.isPlayerVIP(p86) then
    return "VIP"
  end

  return "Free"
end

function sqr31.canTargetPlayer(p87)
  if not p87 or p87 == sqr31.player then
    return false
  end

  if sqr31.isPlayerSafe(p87) then
    return false
  end

  local playerRank = sqr31.getPlayerRank(p87)

  if playerRank == "Owner" then
    return false
  end

  if sqr31.isVIP and playerRank == "VIP" then
    return false
  end

  return true
end

function sqr31.getActiveScriptUsers()
  local list11 = {}

  for _, value77 in pairs(sqr31.scriptUsers or {}) do
    if type(value77) == "table" then
      local v114 = {}

      for key21, value78 in pairs(value77) do
        v114[key21] = value78
      end

      v114.userId = tonumber(v114.userId)
      v114.name = tostring(v114.name or "Unknown")
      v114.displayName = tostring(v114.displayName or v114.name)

      v114.rank = sqr31.getPlayerRankByName and sqr31.getPlayerRankByName(v114.name)
        or f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.owners, v114.name) and "Owner"
        or f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.vips, v114.name) and "VIP" or "Free"

      table.insert(list11, v114)
    end
  end

  table.sort(list11, function(p88, p89) return f77(p88.name) < f77(p89.name) end)
  return list11
end

function sqr31.getPlayerRankByName(p90)
  if f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.owners, p90) then
    return "Owner"
  end

  if f78(sqr31.cloudRecord.vips, p90) then
    return "VIP"
  end

  return "Free"
end

function sqr31.findActiveScriptUser(p91, p92)
  local scriptUsers = p92
  local v115 = f77(p91)
  local v116 = pairs

  if not p92 then
    scriptUsers = sqr31.scriptUsers or {}
  end

  for _, v117 in v116(scriptUsers) do
    if type(v117) == "table" and (f77(v117.name) == v115 or f77(v117.displayName) == v115) then
      return v117
    end
  end
end

local function f80(p93, p94, p95, p96, p97)
  p93.logs = p93.logs or {}

  table.insert(p93.logs, 1, {

    issuerUserId = sqr31.player.UserId,
    issuerRank = sqr31.isOwner and "Owner" or "VIP",

    target = tostring(p95 or ""),
    reason = tostring(p96 or ""),
    extra = tostring(p97 or ""),
  })

  while #p93.logs > 300 do
    table.remove(p93.logs)
  end
end

local function f81()
  return tostring(os.time()) .. "-" .. tostring(math.random(100000, 999999)) .. "-"
    .. tostring(sqr31.player.UserId) .. "-" .. tostring(math.random(1000, 9999))
end

local function f82(p98, p99)
  local v118 = f77(tostring(p99 or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""))

  if v118 == "" then
    return nil
  end

  for _, value79 in pairs(p98.activeUsers or {}) do
    if type(value79) == "table"
      and (f77(value79.name) == v118 or f77(value79.displayName) == v118) then
      return value79
    end
  end

  for _, value80 in ipairs({ "owners", "vips", "bannedUsers" }) do
    for key22, value81 in pairs(p98[value80] or {}) do
      if value81 and f77(key22) == v118 then
        return { name = tostring(key22), userId = 0 }
      end
    end
  end
end

function sqr31.publishRemoteCommand(p100, p101, p102)
  local v119 = f71(f75() or sqr31.cloudRecord or {})
  local isOwner = sqr31.isOwner and "Owner" or sqr31.isVIP and "VIP" or "Free"

  if isOwner == "Free" then
    return false, "No permission"
  end

  local v120 = tostring(p100.name or "")
  local v121 = tonumber(p100.userId) or 0

  if v120 == "" or v121 <= 0 then
    return false, "Target is not available"
  end

  local v122 = f78(v119.owners, v120) and "Owner" or f78(v119.vips, v120) and "VIP" or "Free"

  if f78(v119.safeUsers, v120) then
    return false, "Target is Safe"
  end

  if v122 == "Owner" then
    return false, "Cannot target Owner"
  end

  if isOwner == "VIP" and v122 ~= "Free" then
    return false, "VIP can target Free only"
  end

  if isOwner == "VIP" and f77(p101) == "kick" then
    if v119.day ~= os.date("!%Y-%m-%d") then
      v119.day = os.date("!%Y-%m-%d")
      v119.vipKicks = {}
    end

    local v123 = tonumber(v119.vipKicks[sqr31.player.Name]) or 0

    if v123 >= sqr31.VIP_DAILY_LIMIT then
      return false, "VIP Kick limit: 5/5"
    end

    v119.vipKicks[sqr31.player.Name] = v123 + 1
  end

  local now3 = os.time()
  v119.commands = type(v119.commands) == "table" and v119.commands or {}

  local command = {
    id = f81(),
    createdAt = now3,
    expiresAt = now3 + sqr31.COMMAND_TIMEOUT,
    issuer = sqr31.player.Name,
    issuerUserId = sqr31.player.UserId,
    issuerRank = isOwner,
    target = v120,
    targetUserId = v121,
    command = f77(p101),
    args = { reason = tostring(p102 or "") },
  }

  table.insert(v119.commands, command)

  while #v119.commands > 80 do
    table.remove(v119.commands, 1)
  end

  v119.command = command
  v119.removedUsers = v119.removedUsers or {}
  v119.removedUsers[tostring(v121)] = now3
  v119.activeUsers = v119.activeUsers or {}
  v119.activeUsers[tostring(v121)] = nil

  f80(v119, p101, v120, p102)
  local v124, v125 = f76(v119)

  if not v124 then
    return false, v125
  end

  return true, nil
end

function sqr31.setVIPByName(p103, p104)
  local v126 = f71(f75() or {})
  local v127 = f82(v126, p103)

  if not v127 then
    return false, "User not found"
  end

  local v128 = tostring(v127.name)

  if f78(v126.owners, v128) then
    return false, "Owner cannot be VIP"
  end

  if p104 then
    v126.vips[v128] = true
    v126.bannedUsers[v128] = nil
    f80(v126, "VIP", v128, "Promoted to VIP")
  else
    v126.vips[v128] = nil
    f80(v126, "UnVIP", v128, "VIP removed")
  end

  local v129, v130 = f76(v126)

  if v129 then
    f79(v126)
  end

  return v129, v130
end

function sqr31.setBanByName(p105, p106, p107)
  local v131 = f71(f75() or {})
  local v132 = f82(v131, p105)

  if not v132 then
    return false, "User not found"
  end

  local v133 = tostring(v132.name)

  if f78(v131.owners, v133) then
    return false, "Cannot ban Owner"
  end

  if p106 then
    v131.bannedUsers[v133] = true
    f80(v131, "Ban", v133, p107)
    local v134 = tonumber(v132.userId) or 0

    if v134 > 0 then
      local now4 = os.time()
      v131.commands = type(v131.commands) == "table" and v131.commands or {}

      local command2 = {
        id = f81(),
        createdAt = now4,
        expiresAt = now4 + sqr31.COMMAND_TIMEOUT,
        issuer = sqr31.player.Name,
        issuerUserId = sqr31.player.UserId,
        issuerRank = "Owner",
        target = v133,
        targetUserId = v134,
        command = "ban",
        args = { reason = tostring(p107 or "Banned by SQR") },
      }

      table.insert(v131.commands, command2)

      while #v131.commands > 80 do
        table.remove(v131.commands, 1)
      end

      v131.command = command2
      v131.removedUsers = v131.removedUsers or {}
      v131.removedUsers[tostring(v134)] = now4
      v131.activeUsers = v131.activeUsers or {}
      v131.activeUsers[tostring(v134)] = nil
    end
  else
    v131.bannedUsers[v133] = nil
    f80(v131, "UnBan", v133, p107 or "Ban removed")
  end

  local v135, v136 = f76(v131)

  if v135 then
    f79(v131)
  end

  return v135, v136
end

function sqr31.setOwnerByName(p108, p109)
  local v137 = f71(f75() or {})

    return false, "Only original Owners can manage Owner rank"
  end

  if not p109 and (v140 == "nanmewelldoit" or v140 == "nanneverlet") then
    return false, "Original Owner cannot be removed"
  end

  if p109 then
    v137.owners[v139] = true
    v137.vips[v139] = nil

  else
    v137.owners[v139] = nil
    v137.vips[v139] = nil

  end

  local v142, v143 = f76(v137)

  if v142 then
    f79(v137)
  end

  return v142, v143
end

task.spawn(function()
  while sqr31.remoteCommandAlive do
    local v153 = f75()

    if v153 then
      local v154 = f71(v153)
      f79(v154)
      local now5 = os.time()
      local commands = type(v154.commands) == "table" and v154.commands or {}

      if #commands == 0 and type(v154.command) == "table" then
        commands = { v154.command }
      end

      for _, value82 in ipairs(commands) do
        local v155 = tostring(value82.id or "")
        local v156 = tonumber(value82.createdAt) or 0
        local v157 = tonumber(value82.expiresAt) or v156 + sqr31.COMMAND_TIMEOUT

        if v155 ~= "" and not sqr31.processedCommands[v155] and v156 >= sqr31.scriptStartTime
          and now5 <= v157 then
          sqr31.processedCommands[v155] = now5
        end
      end

      if sqr31.isBanned then
        sqr31.player:Kick("Reason : Banned from using this script")
        break
      end
    end

    task.wait(sqr31.JSON_POLL)
  end
end)

  end
end)

local function f84(instance11)
  for _, value83 in ipairs(instance11:GetChildren()) do
    if not value83:IsA("UIListLayout") and not value83:IsA("UIPadding") then
      pcall(function() value83:Destroy() end)
    end
  end
end

local vip = f83("VIP")

if vip then
  vip.ClipsDescendants = true

  sqr32.vipStatus = makeTextLabel(vip, "VIP • Kick: 0/5", 8, 24, 15)
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -45)
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 38)
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 21, 30)
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 5
  sqr32.vipUsersFrame.Parent = vip

  Instance.new("UICorner", sqr32.vipUsersFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

  sqr32.vipLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", sqr32.vipUsersFrame)
  sqr32.vipLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
  sqr32.vipItems = {}

  function sqr32.vipRefresh()
    if not (sqr32.isVIP or sqr32.isOwner) then
      return
    end

    local v161 = {}
    local list12 = {}

    for _, value84 in ipairs((sqr32.getActiveScriptUsers())) do
      if value84.userId and value84.userId ~= sqr32.player.UserId then
        v161[tostring(value84.userId)] = true
        sqr32.vipKnownUsers[tostring(value84.userId)] = value84

        if value84.rank == "Free" then
          list12[#list12 + 1] = tostring(value84.userId) .. ":" .. tostring(value84.name) .. ":"
            .. tostring(value84.rank)
        end
      end
    end

    for key23 in pairs(sqr32.vipKnownUsers) do
      if not v161[key23] and not (sqr32.cloudRecord.activeUsers or {})[key23] then
        sqr32.vipKnownUsers[key23] = nil
      end
    end

    table.sort(list12)
    local joined = table.concat(list12, "|")
    local v162 = tonumber((sqr32.cloudRecord.vipKicks or {})[sqr32.player.Name])
    sqr32.vipStatus.Text = "VIP • Kick: 5/" .. math.min(v162 or 0, 5)

    if joined == sqr32.vipUiSignature and sqr32.vipUsersFrame:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame") then
      return
    end

    sqr32.vipUiSignature = joined
    f84(sqr32.vipUsersFrame)
    sqr32.vipItems = {}
    local count3 = 0

    for _, value85 in pairs(sqr32.vipKnownUsers) do
      if value85.userId and value85.userId ~= sqr32.player.UserId and value85.rank == "Free" then
        count3 = count3 + 1

        local frame11 = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame11.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 72)
        frame11.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 39, 52)
        frame11.Parent = sqr32.vipUsersFrame

        Instance.new("UICorner", frame11).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)

        local imageLabel5 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        imageLabel5.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 54)
        imageLabel5.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 9)
        imageLabel5.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        imageLabel5.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        imageLabel5.Parent = frame11

        imageLabel5.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(value85.userId)
          .. "&w=150&h=150"

        task.spawn(function()
          pcall(function()
            local getUserThumbnailAsync = sqr32.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
              tonumber(value85.userId), Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust,
              Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
            )

            if imageLabel5 and imageLabel5.Parent and getUserThumbnailAsync
              and getUserThumbnailAsync ~= "" then
              imageLabel5.Image = getUserThumbnailAsync
            end
          end)
        end)

        local textLabel19 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        textLabel19.Size = UDim2.new(1, -155, 0, 25)
        textLabel19.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 9)
        textLabel19.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textLabel19.Text = "@" .. value85.name
        textLabel19.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        textLabel19.TextSize = 13
        textLabel19.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        textLabel19.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        textLabel19.Parent = frame11

        local textLabel20 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        textLabel20.Size = UDim2.new(1, -155, 0, 20)
        textLabel20.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 34)
        textLabel20.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textLabel20.Text = "UserId: " .. tostring(value85.userId)
        textLabel20.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 170, 180)
        textLabel20.TextSize = 10
        textLabel20.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        textLabel20.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        textLabel20.Parent = frame11

        local textButton18 = makeTextButton3(
          frame11, "KICK", 0, 19, 68, Color3.fromRGB(180, 55, 55)
        )

        textButton18.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 19)

        textButton18.Activated:Connect(function()
          if not sqr32.isOwner then
            if (tonumber((sqr32.cloudRecord.vipKicks or {})[sqr32.player.Name]) or 0) >= 5 then
              sqr32.ShowRankMessage("VIP Kick limit: 5/5")
              return
            end
          end

          local v163, v164 = sqr32.publishRemoteCommand({
            name = value85.name,
            userId = value85.userId,
          }, "kick", "")

          if v163 then
            sqr32.vipKnownUsers[tostring(value85.userId)] = nil
            sqr32.ownerKnownUsers[tostring(value85.userId)] = nil
          end

          sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v163 and "Kick sent to @" .. value85.name
            or tostring(v164 or "Kick failed"))
        end)
      end
    end

    if count3 == 0 then
      makeTextLabel(
        sqr32.vipUsersFrame, "لا يوجد Free مستخدم للسكربت حالياً", 8,
        30, 12
      )
    end

    sqr32.vipUsersFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.max(45, count3 * 78 + 12))
  end
end

      or "Safe", 0, 64, 58, Color3.fromRGB(70, 100, 145))

    textButton27.Position = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 75)

    local textButton28 = makeTextButton3(frame17, text29.rank == "Owner" and "UnOwner" or "Owner", 0, 43, 72, text29.rank == "Owner" and Color3.fromRGB(190, 55, 55)
      or Color3.fromRGB(45, 140, 75))

    textButton28.Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 43)

    textButton24.Activated:Connect(function()
      local name13 = text29.name
      local userId = text29.userId
      local text30 = textBox6.Text

      textButton24.Active = false
      textButton24.Text = "..."

      task.spawn(function()
        local v170, v171 = sqr32.publishRemoteCommand(
          { name = name13, userId = userId }, "kick", text30
        )

        pcall(function()
          if textButton24 and textButton24.Parent then
            textButton24.Active = true
            textButton24.Text = "KICK"
          end
        end)

        if v170 then
          sqr32.vipKnownUsers[tostring(userId)] = nil
          sqr32.ownerKnownUsers[tostring(userId)] = nil
        end

        sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v170 and "Kick sent to @" .. name13
          or tostring(v171 or "Kick failed"))
      end)
    end)

    textButton25.Activated:Connect(function()
      local v172, v173 = sqr32.setBanByName(text29.name, true, textBox6.Text)

      sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v172 and "Ban sent to @" .. text29.name
        or tostring(v173 or "Ban failed"))
    end)

    textButton26.Activated:Connect(function()
      if text29.rank == "Owner" then
        return
      end

      local v174, v175 = sqr32.setVIPByName(text29.name, text29.rank ~= "VIP")
      local v176 = v174 and "Rank updated"
      sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v176 or tostring(v175 or "Failed"))
    end)

    textButton27.Activated:Connect(function()
      local isPlayerSafe2 = sqr32.isPlayerSafe and sqr32.isPlayerSafe({ Name = text29.name })
        or false

      local v177, v178 = sqr32.setSafeByName(text29.name, not isPlayerSafe2)

      if v177 then
        textButton27.Text = not isPlayerSafe2 and "UnSafe" or "Safe"
      end

      local v179 = v177 and "Safe updated"
      sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v179 or tostring(v178 or "Safe failed"))
    end)

    textButton28.Activated:Connect(function()
      textButton28.Active = false
      local v180 = text29.rank ~= "Owner"
      textButton28.Text = v180 and "..." or "..."

      task.spawn(function()
        local v181, v182 = sqr32.setOwnerByName(text29.name, v180)

        pcall(function()
          if textButton28 and textButton28.Parent then
            textButton28.Active = true
            textButton28.Text = v180 and "Owner" or "UnOwner"
          end
        end)

        sqr32.ShowRankMessage(v181 and (v180 and "Owner added" or "Owner removed")
          or tostring(v182 or "Owner update failed"))

        task.defer(function() pcall(function() sqr32.ownerRefresh() end) end)
      end)
    end)

    return frame17
  end

  function sqr32.ownerRefresh()
    if not sqr32.isOwner then
      return
    end

    local activeScriptUsers = sqr32.getActiveScriptUsers()
    local v183 = {}

    for _, value91 in ipairs(activeScriptUsers) do
      if tonumber(value91.userId) ~= sqr32.player.UserId then
        v183[tostring(value91.userId)] = true
        sqr32.ownerKnownUsers[tostring(value91.userId)] = value91
      end
    end

    local v184 = {}

    for _, value92 in ipairs(sqr32.Players:GetPlayers()) do
      v184[tostring(value92.UserId)] = true
    end

    for key26 in pairs(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers) do
      if not v184[key26] then
        sqr32.ownerKnownUsers[key26] = nil
      elseif not v183[key26] and not (sqr32.cloudRecord.activeUsers or {})[key26] then
        sqr32.ownerKnownUsers[key26] = nil
      end
    end

    local list13 = {}

    for key27, value93 in pairs(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers) do
      list13[#list13 + 1] = tostring(key27) .. ":" .. tostring(value93.name) .. ":"
        .. tostring(value93.rank)
    end

    table.sort(list13)
    local joined2 = table.concat(list13, "|")

    sqr32.ownerUserCount.Text = "Script Users: " .. tostring(math.max(
      #activeScriptUsers, table.getn(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers)
    ))

    local count6 = 0

    for _, value94 in pairs(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers) do
      if tonumber(value94.userId) ~= sqr32.player.UserId then
        count6 = count6 + 1
      end
    end

    if joined2 ~= sqr32.ownerUiSignature then
      sqr32.ownerUiSignature = joined2
      f84(sqr32.ownerPlayerFrame)

      for _, value95 in pairs(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers) do
        if tonumber(value95.userId) ~= sqr32.player.UserId then
          f87(value95)
        end
      end

      if count6 == 0 then
        makeTextLabel(
          sqr32.ownerPlayerFrame,
          "لا يوجد مستخدمون آخرون للسكربت حالياً", 8, 30, 12
        )
      end

      sqr32.ownerPlayerFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.max(45, count6 * 124 + 10))
    end

    f84(sqr32.ownerResetFrame)
    local count7 = 0

    for _, value96 in pairs(sqr32.ownerKnownUsers) do
      if value96.rank == "VIP" then
        count7 = count7 + 1

        local frame18 = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame18.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 36)
        frame18.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 38, 50)
        frame18.Parent = sqr32.ownerResetFrame

        Instance.new("UICorner", frame18).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
        local v185 = tonumber((sqr32.cloudRecord.vipKicks or {})[value96.name])

        local textLabel26 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        textLabel26.Size = UDim2.new(1, -85, 1, 0)
        textLabel26.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0)
        textLabel26.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        textLabel26.Text = "@" .. value96.name .. " • VIP • Kick: 5/"
          .. math.min(v185 or 0, 5)

        textLabel26.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        textLabel26.TextSize = 11
        textLabel26.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        textLabel26.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        textLabel26.Parent = frame18

        local textButton29 = makeTextButton3(
          frame18, "RESET", 0, 1, 65, Color3.fromRGB(45, 130, 85)
        )

        textButton29.Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 1)
        textButton29.Activated:Connect(function() sqr32.resetVIPKicks(value96.name) end)
      end
    end

    if count7 == 0 then
      makeTextLabel(sqr32.ownerResetFrame, "لا يوجد VIP حالياً", 8, 30, 12)
    end

    sqr32.ownerResetFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.max(42, count7 * 40 + 8))
    owner.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 485 + math.max(0, count6 * 124))
  end
end

task.spawn(function()
  task.wait(0.05)

  pcall(function()
    if sqr32.refreshSectionVisibility then
      sqr32.refreshSectionVisibility()
    end

    if sqr32.vipRefresh then
      sqr32.vipRefresh()
    end

    if sqr32.ownerRefresh then
      sqr32.ownerRefresh()
    end
  end)

  while sqr32.remoteCommandAlive do
    task.wait(1)

    pcall(function()
      if sqr32.refreshSectionVisibility then
        sqr32.refreshSectionVisibility()
      end

      if sqr32.vipRefresh then
        sqr32.vipRefresh()
      end

      if sqr32.ownerRefresh then
        sqr32.ownerRefresh()
      end
    end)
  end
end)

local sqr33 = _G.SQR

local function f88()
  if sqr33.refreshSectionVisibility then
    pcall(sqr33.refreshSectionVisibility)
  end

  if sqr33.screenGui and sqr33.screenGui.Enabled and sqr33.mainFrame then
    sqr33.mainFrame.Visible = true
  end

  if sqr33.sidebar and sqr33.screenGui and sqr33.screenGui.Enabled then
    sqr33.sidebar.Visible = true
  end

  local instance13 = sqr33.sectionFrames[sqr33.currentSection]

  if instance13 and sqr33.sectionAllowed(sqr33.sections[sqr33.currentSection]) then
    instance13.Visible = true
    instance13.ZIndex = 4

    if instance13:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
      instance13.ScrollBarThickness = 5
    end

    for _, value97 in ipairs(instance13:GetDescendants()) do
      if value97:IsA("GuiButton") or value97:IsA("TextBox") or value97:IsA("TextLabel")
        or value97:IsA("ImageLabel") or value97:IsA("ImageButton") then
        value97.ZIndex = math.max(value97.ZIndex, 6)
      end
    end
  end
end

function sqr33.setScriptAvatar(instance14, p121)
  local v186 = tonumber(p121)

  if not instance14 or not v186 or v186 <= 0 then
    return
  end

  instance14.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(v186) .. "&w=150&h=150"

  task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
      local getUserThumbnailAsync3 = sqr33.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
        v186, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
      )

      if instance14 and instance14.Parent and getUserThumbnailAsync3
        and getUserThumbnailAsync3 ~= "" then
        instance14.Image = getUserThumbnailAsync3
      end
    end)
  end)
end

f88()

task.spawn(function()
  while sqr33.remoteCommandAlive do
    task.wait(3)

    pcall(function()
      if sqr33.screenGui and not sqr33.screenGui.Parent and sqr33.playerGui then
        sqr33.screenGui.Parent = sqr33.playerGui
      end

      if sqr33.screenGui and sqr33.screenGui.Enabled and sqr33.mainFrame
        and not sqr33.mainFrame.Parent then
        sqr33.mainFrame.Parent = sqr33.screenGui
      end

      for _, value98 in ipairs(sqr33.sectionFrames) do
        if value98 and not value98.Parent and sqr33.mainFrame then
          value98.Parent = sqr33.mainFrame
        end
      end

      if sqr33.screenGui and sqr33.screenGui.Enabled then
        f88()
      end
    end)
  end
end)

local sqr34 = _G.SQR
sqr34.installButtonFeedback(sqr34.gui)
sqr34.installButtonFeedback(sqr34.screenGui)
sqr34.closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
sqr34.closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
sqr34.closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -35, 0, 2)
sqr34.closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
sqr34.closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
sqr34.closeBtn.Text = "X"
sqr34.closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
sqr34.closeBtn.TextScaled = true
sqr34.closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
sqr34.closeBtn.Parent = sqr34.mainFrame

Instance.new("UICorner", sqr34.closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

sqr34.closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
  pcall(function() sqr34.saveSettings() end)
  sqr34.screenGui.Enabled = false

  pcall(function()
    sqr34.TweenService:Create(sqr34.uiScale, TweenInfo.new(
      0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In
    ), { Scale = 0.01 }):Play()
  end)

  wait(0.27)
  local clamped15 = math.clamp(tonumber(sqr34.settingState.uiScale) or 1, 0.7, 1.3)

  sqr34.uiScale.Scale = clamped15
  sqr34.mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -300 * clamped15, 0.5, -200 * clamped15)
  sqr34.mainFrame.Visible = false
end)

pcall(function()
  sqr34.gameBindToClose = game:BindToClose(function()
    pcall(function() sqr34.saveSettings() end)
  end)
end)

print("SQR UI loaded successfully!")
